Collapsed duplicate clusters `5229/5330`, `4982/5014`, `4788/4832`, `2758/3655`, `4957/5239`, `5116/5210`, `5186/5187`; repaired venue fields for `4978`-`4981`, `4060`, `5345`, `5404`; rebuilt affected Telegraph/month surfaces and documented evidence in the incident record. Left `4870` review-only because no source-grounded venue was available.
- **LLM / gpt-4o daily spend guard**: added a shared wrapper-level cap for `gpt-4o` requests. `FOUR_O_GPT4O_DAILY_TOKEN_LIMIT` defaults to `950000`; before sending `gpt-4o`, the bot checks today's `token_usage` total when Supabase is available (falling back to in-process counters) and routes the request to `FOUR_O_GPT4O_FALLBACK_MODEL` (`gpt-4o-mini`) if the estimated call would cross the cap. Covered by an `ask_4o` fallback regression test and documented in `docs/llm/request-guide.md` / `.env.example`.
- **Partner promo — multi-activity in one campaign (2026-05-26)**: the campaign card now has a `➕ Активность` button that opens a condensed FSM (surface → slot → count → confirm) reusing the existing creation steps but skipping end-date and mode — both inherit from the campaign. Backed by `promo.add_partner_activity_to_campaign(PartnerActivitySpec)`; archived campaigns refuse new activities with an explicit "сначала восстановите" message. The card's "Активности:" section now lists every PromoActivity row with human-readable labels: technical keys (`video_general`, `popular_review`, `first_two_slots`) are translated through `_SURFACE_LABELS` / `_PROFILE_LABELS` / `_SLOT_POLICY_LABELS` and `_humanize_activity` to "🎬 Видеоанонс · Популярное · слот 1–2 · всего показов: 3". Live E2E verified end-to-end via @The_day_of_kk → @eventsbotTestBot on an isolated prod-snapshot copy: create campaign through 🎬, add second activity through ➕ Активность, card renders both with human labels. Added unit tests for `add_partner_activity_to_campaign` (happy path, archived rejection, vk_repost surface), card-keyboard tests (button shown when active / hidden when archived), and a card-text assertion that both activities appear with the new labels and no raw technical keys leak. Updated the canonical spec (`docs/features/promo-campaigns/partner-promo.md`) with §6.1 add-activity flow, Gherkin scenarios for the multi-activity card, and the human-label contract.
- **Guide excursions / VK digest MVP (2026-05-19)**: added postponed VK wall-post fanout for the latest successful guide `new_occurrences` digest issue to `vk.com/uhtykaliningrad` (`ENABLE_GUIDE_DIGEST_VK`, `GUIDE_DIGEST_VK_TARGET`, `/guide_digest_vk`). The VK render is one plain-text post with first-line count + months, uses `vk.cc` shortening for Telegram registration/source links, stores VK evidence in `guide_digest_issue.published_targets_json`, and reuses the shared `post_to_vk` 10-minute postponed queue/community-author contract. Added guide VK source seeds for `vk.com/ruin.keepers` and `vk.com/narodexcursovod`; documented the rollout pitfalls and smoke checks.
- **VK postponed publishing (2026-05-19)**: new outgoing VK community wall posts now go through postponed publication instead of immediate wall publishing. Shared `post_to_vk` reserves `publish_date` in `Europe/Kaliningrad`, keeps at least a 10-minute gap from now and from the latest already postponed post in the target community, and moves night slots before 06:00 to 06:00. The existing community-author contract (`owner_id=-<group_id>`, `from_group=1`, `signed=0`) is preserved; old posts are not changed. Added regression tests and documented the runtime knobs in `docs/features/vk-publishing/README.md`.
- **Incident / VK posts authored by personal user (INC-2026-05-19)**: fixed shared VK wall publishing so future `kenigeventsofficial` daily posts and `klgdevents` event posts are authored by the target community, not by the admin user. `post_to_vk` now always sends `from_group=1` and `signed=0` together with `owner_id=-<group_id>`, including the group-token actor path (`VK_TOKEN` / `VK_TOKEN_AFISHA`) that production uses first. Existing posts are intentionally left untouched per operator request. Added a regression test for group-token `wall.post` params and documented the authoring/smoke contract in `docs/features/vk-publishing/README.md`.
- **Partner promo — phase A (2026-05-18)**: shipped the partner-promo creation flow and the shared button-rich management menu that the prod incident `INC-2026-05-18` had to defang. The `🎬` button on `/events` now opens the 6-step FSM (surface → slot → count → end-date → mode → confirm) for the event-owning partner or any superadmin, replacing the never-used `video_include_count` counter. The flow creates a `PromoCampaign`+`PromoTarget`+`PromoActivity` via `promo.create_partner_event_promo_campaign`; campaign `ends_at` is clamped to `event.end_date or event.date`, partner default priority is `2`, superadmin default `1`. New `selection_policy=first_two_slots` constant is recognised by the creation path (resolver integration is part of phase B). New tables: `organization` (seeded with `Научная библиотека` — the canonical name that matches `user.organization` for @Ekaterina_Tikun — with `vk_source_group_ids=[30777579]`, `video_profile_key='konb'`, `sponsorship_default='Партнёрский материал · Научная библиотека'`) and `promo_vk_repost_job` (planner rows; runner ships in phase B). `/promo` (no args) now opens a button-rich menu shared by admin and partner: partner sees only their own non-archived campaigns, admin sees all plus a `Seed 80` shortcut; each row opens a campaign card with Pause/Resume, Archive, Stats, Rename, and (admin only) Priority `P0..P3`. Stats screen reads `promo_exposure`. Rename uses a TTL-scoped input session. VK-repost surface is wired but currently answers "появится в следующем релизе" until the scheduler/runner lands; the Site button is a documented placeholder alert. Added focused tests under `tests/test_partner_promo.py` and `tests/test_partner_promo_menu.py` (21 cases) and verified existing `tests/test_promo.py` (9 cases) still pass; the `create_app()` startup smoke from `INC-2026-05-18` continues to pass with the new modules in place. Promoted the spec from `docs/backlog/features/promo-campaigns/partner-promo.md` (now a redirect stub) to canonical `docs/features/promo-campaigns/partner-promo.md` with Gherkin scenarios covering FSM happy path, role authorization, end-date clamp, editorial mode, KONB-CherryFlash auto-promote rule (phase B), and the INC-2026-05-18 startup regression. The canonical spec also documents two phase-B additions surfaced by the operator this round: (1) the KONB-CherryFlash auto-promote rule — events of `Научная библиотека` that have a `guaranteed_any_position` campaign are lifted to slot 1–2 in KONB CherryFlash unless a higher-priority `first_slot`/`first_two_slots` partner-library campaign already occupies those slots; (2) `vk_repost` is now an accepted activity type in the schema (button visibility, click-time alert, and DB row creation will require the future runner) with the planner table already in place. Added an interface-only dry-run script `scripts/partner_promo_interface_dry_run.py` that walks 15 frames of the partner flow against a synthetic Tikun profile for visual verification before deploy.
- **Kenigsberg poetry stories — voice trim regression (2026-05-18)**: `--poetry-test` cut the voice-over a few seconds short twice in a row. Root cause: `render_frames` overrides the voice-derived `main_duration_target` with `slots[-1][1]` from `rhythm_slots_from_strong_beats`, and the rhythm builder's "extend to target_duration" fallback was gated on `current < MIN_STRONG_MAIN_DURATION (15s)` — a threshold sized for 18s thought stories. For a 55s voice, the strong-beat loop exhausted anchors at ~52s, the 15s gate blocked the tail slot, and `encode_video` trimmed the voice at 52s. Fix: added `extend_to_duration: bool = False` to `rhythm_slots_from_strong_beats`, `approximate_rhythm_slots`, and `beat_slots`. `main()` passes the flag when poetry voice is present, so the last slot always reaches `voice_duration` (appending a tail slot when the leftover is ≥0.45s, otherwise extending the last slot). Thoughts-mode behavior is unchanged (the strong-beat ending contract still applies without the flag). Added regression tests covering the 55s voice case, the unchanged thoughts-mode strict ending, and the approximate-fallback extension. Updated `docs/features/kenigsberg-stories/README.md` to document that voice-driven main duration is a hard constraint.
- **Kenigsberg poetry stories — round-2 test-run fixes (2026-05-18)**: two render bugs found in the post-round-1 `--poetry-test` output. (a) **Title cover invisible on frame 0.** `draw_poem_block` still computed `alpha = 232 * in_p * (1-out_p)` for the title branch, so at t=0 `in_p=0` → fully transparent stripe, and the first frame (the Telegram thumbnail) was just black. Also, the title cue itself started at `start_pad=0.18`, leaving the first ~5 frames empty regardless. Fix: title cues now start at timeline t=0 (`start_pad` is set to 0 when the first block is single-line), and the title branch holds full alpha through the cue with only a 0.24s fade-out at the end. (b) **Author signature glued to the last quatrain.** The inter-group gap (`group_extra`) was applied to `cursor_y` AFTER drawing the line, so for groups `[[L1..L4],[S1,S2]]` the extra landed between S1 and S2 instead of between L4 and S1 — the user saw "5 lines stuck + gap + 1 line" instead of "4 lines + gap + 2 lines". Fix: factored layout into a pure `poem_line_y_positions(...)` helper that inserts the extra BEFORE the first line of each new group; `draw_poem_block` now uses those positions verbatim. Added regression tests: title cue starts at 0 / non-title cue keeps the start pad; layout helper exercised against 4+2 and 4+3 signature shapes plus the no-group baseline; an image-level smoke test confirming the title cue paints non-background pixels at t=0.
- **Kenigsberg poetry stories — round-1 test-run feedback (2026-05-18)**: applied the three refinements from `docs/backlog/features/cherryflash/KenigsberPoetry.md` after the first `/kenigsberg --poetry-test` run. (a) **Per-poem footage dataset pinning.** Added optional `video_dataset` metadata to `poems.md` blocks. When set, `choose_video_dataset` is forced to that dataset (and fail-closed if it is not mounted), so a poem can stay visually anchored to a single period; absent value keeps the legacy weighted random pick. `poem-1` is pinned to `koenigsberg19191940`. (b) **Title-card style for 1-line opening blocks.** `build_poetry_text_cues` marks a first block with one line as `style="title"`, and `draw_poem_block` renders that cue at full stripe width and full alpha from t=0 (with a short fade-in) instead of the per-line slide-in animation, so the title reads as the video heading right away. (c) **Last quatrain shown together with the author signature.** Handler now extracts visible signature lines (`author` + `author_note`, dropping `@handle`), strips the trailing author-only block from `poem_blocks`, and forwards `poem_signature_lines`. The final cue carries `style="final"` and `groups=[last_stanza, signature]`; `draw_poem_block` lays out both groups in one block with an extra inter-group gap, so the signature stays on screen for the whole final stanza window. Updated `docs/features/kenigsberg-stories/README.md`; added regression tests for `video_dataset` parsing, payload signature stripping, cue style markup, and forced-dataset pinning (including fail-closed when the pinned dataset is missing).
- **Kenigsberg poetry stories (2026-05-18)**: added poetry mode for `/kenigsberg` with `docs/features/kenigsberg-stories/poems.md`, `/kenigsberg --poetry-test` test publication to `@keniggpt`, and `/kenigsberg --poetry-today` forced production poetry run. Scheduled Kenigsberg now automatically picks poetry every 3 days when poems are available and retries the same pending poem after failed publication; test publications do not consume the poem or move production cadence. Kaggle rendering can mount `zigomaro/kenigsberg-audio`, match files like `-poem-1-enhanced-v2.mp3`, sync poem blocks to a voice-over, keep poem lines unwrapped, mix background music very low under normalized voice with adaptive gain for higher `voice_risk` tracks, and shorten the outro under Telegram's 60s native story cap so a 55s voice-over still keeps the `МОСТ / В КЁНИГСБЕРГ` outro screen. Poetry production also adds a best-effort VK wall post to `vk.com/mostvkenig` with an LLM-generated hook, author mention lines, and fixed Kenigsberg hashtags.
- **Incident / KОНБ CherryFlash missed prod slot (INC-2026-05-18)**: fixed two production blockers for `partner_konb_library_001`. Partner-track render checks are now scoped by `profile_key`, so a slow `popular_review_eco` render no longer blocks the KОНБ slot scheduled 7 minutes later. KОНБ selection also has a last-resort `konb_recycle` pool: if fresh popularity and future fallback underfill, current/future events from prior KОНБ video issues may repeat after a Europe/Kaliningrad calendar-day boundary. The KОНБ partner pipeline can now opt into an even later emergency same-day recycle layer (`source_window=konb_same_day_recycle`, `anti_repeat_status=same_day_recycle`) so a production slot is not missed when the only usable current events already appeared in earlier same-day test/compensation runs. Same-video duplicates remain blocked, and same-day repeats remain blocked by default for normal selector calls. Added regression tests for scoped render locks, recycle fallback, default same-day repeat blocking, and KОНБ emergency same-day recycle; documented the incident contract and updated the partner-track schedule/retry docs.
- **VK publishing polish + cherryflash free-label fix + Geo-school VK source (2026-05-18)**:
  - **Fixed `cherryflash` "БЕСПЛАТНО" leak.** `_format_display_price` in `scripts/render_cherryflash_full.py` defaulted to "БЕСПЛАТНО" when there was no objective price info — a round-1 KONB override (library events are mostly free) that incorrectly leaked into the general cherryflash announce, so most events on the 2026-05-18 video showed "бесплатно" without DB evidence. Now: only return "БЕСПЛАТНО" when `is_free` is true or `pmin`/`pmax` are explicitly zero; otherwise return empty string (price block is skipped by the existing `if scene.price_line` guard in `_build_primary_blocks`).
  - **VK source-post photo fallback to Telegraph.** `sync_vk_source_post` in `main_part2.py` now backfills attachments from the event's Telegraph page (`extract_telegraph_image_urls(event.telegraph_url)`) when `event.photo_urls` is empty. Fixes the `wall-231920894_1088` symptom (telegra.ph/Rasprav-krylya-05-04-2 shows two images, VK post had none). The fallback only runs when the existing VK post has no photo attachments yet (probe via `wall.getById`) so repeat Smart Update syncs do not re-upload duplicate photos. Telegraph images are downloaded by `upload_vk_photo` and re-uploaded to the target group as native VK photos.
  - **Multi-photo VK posts now render as a grid.** Added `primary_attachments_mode=grid` to `wall.post` in `post_to_vk` and `_vkrev_handle_repost`, to `wall.edit` in `edit_vk_post`, and to the shortpost publish flow `_vkrev_publish_shortpost`. Triggered only when `len(attachments)` / `len(photos)` / `len(photo_attachments) > 1`. Replaces the default carousel rendering in modern VK clients so subscribers see all photos at once.
  - **Smart Update report shows VK post URL.** `AddedEventInfo` / `UpdatedEventInfo` (in `source_parsing/handlers.py`) and `TelegramMonitorEventInfo` (in `source_parsing/telegram/handlers.py`) gained an optional `vk_post_url` field, populated from `event.source_vk_post_url`. Rendered as a new `VK: пост` line after `ICS:` in three reports: `source_parsing/handlers.py` (Markdown), `vk_auto_queue.py` (HTML), `source_parsing/telegram/service.py` (HTML). `refresh_telegram_monitor_event_info` also refreshes the URL.
  - **Added `https://vk.com/club194393485` (Географическая школа) to VK community monitoring.** Idempotent `INSERT OR IGNORE INTO vk_source(group_id, screen_name, name)` seed in `db.init` next to the existing location seeds, so the source appears on next bot start and survives DB refresh. Source: operator request 2026-05-18.
  - **VK personal pages in monitoring.** `vk_source.owner_type` now distinguishes communities (`group`, negative wall owner id) from personal pages (`user`, positive wall owner id), and VK crawl/review/import/repost URL handling respects it. Seeded `vk.com/ivsguide` and `vk.ru/natakkaz` as `owner_type='user'` alongside the Geo-school community source. Unit tests can opt out of these default seeds with `DB_INIT_SKIP_VK_SOURCES_SEED=1`.
- **Guide excursions / VK publics in Kaggle monitoring (2026-05-18)**: guide source seeding now supports `platform='vk'` and adds `vk.ru/balticsyndicate` plus `vk.com/konb39` to the Kaggle guide-monitoring path. The Kaggle config now ships enabled Telegram and VK sources with `platform/source_url`, the encrypted secrets payload passes `GUIDE_MONITORING_VK_TOKEN` from the dedicated guide/Kaggle VK token env (`GUIDE_MONITORING_VK_TOKEN`, or `GUIDE_MONITORING_VK_TOKEN_ENV` defaulting to `VK_ACCESS_TOKEN5`), and the notebook runner fetches VK wall posts through VK API before the same guide screen/extract pipeline.
- **VK daily / today date line (2026-05-18)**: the morning `НЕ ПРОПУСТИТЕ СЕГОДНЯ` VK daily section now prints a separate full-date line under the header while keeping event rows one-line with time prefixes and VK post links.
- **Incident / prod startup missing partner promo module (INC-2026-05-18)**: guarded the optional partner-promo startup and lazy handler imports so a missing local-only `partner_promo` module cannot crash Fly production during `create_app()`; unavailable partner-promo callbacks now fail closed with an operator-visible message. `.dockerignore` now excludes `.venv/` so local test environments do not inflate emergency deploy contexts.
- **VK daily / compact `/daily` rows (2026-05-18)**: morning `НЕ ПРОПУСТИТЕ СЕГОДНЯ` rows now show event time instead of the current date, while evening `ДОБАВИЛИ В АНОНС` rows keep the future event date. VK daily title links now prefer the managed `vk.com/klgdevents` event post (`vk_repost_url`) and fall back to source VK wall posts (`source_post_url` / `source_vk_post_url`), including partner-created events, so both morning and evening VK daily posts expose links for most imported events.
- **Kenigsberg stories → VK community story fanout to `vk.com/mostvkenig` (2026-05-18)**: `/kenigsberg` (manual + scheduled `kenigsberg_story_daily` at `19:30 Europe/Kaliningrad`) now also publishes the generated story as a VK community story to `vk.com/mostvkenig`. Added a fourth entry to `_kenigsberg_story_targets()` in `handlers/kenigsberg_stories_cmd.py` with `peer=mostvkenig`, `transport=vk_story`, `mode=upload`, `delay_seconds=120`, `blocking=False`, `required=False`, `label=vk:mostvkenig:story`. The shared Kaggle helper already supported `transport=vk_story` (validated 2026-05-17 by the CherryFlash KONB round-3 prod launch publishing to `konb39`); the encrypted runtime bundle already ships `vk_access_token` from env `VK_ACCESS_TOKEN5`, so no new env var or secret rotation is needed. The VK target is intentionally best-effort: failure (missing admin rights in `mostvkenig`, VK upload errors, etc.) is logged in `story_publish_report.json` and must not block or fail the existing Telegram publish to `@mostvkenig` / `@loving_guide39` / `@jane_tour39`. Updated `tests/test_kenigsberg_stories.py::test_kenigsberg_production_story_config_uses_mostvkenig_and_native_profile` to assert the fourth target. Updated `docs/features/kenigsberg-stories/README.md` (renamed `Telegram publish contract` → `Publish contract`, documented the VK fanout).
- **VK publishing / klgdevents event post regression + VK weekly/weekend nav retirement (2026-05-18)**: fixed two regressions surfaced by the 2026-05-18 prod logs (`/data/runtime_logs/events-bot.log`) — Smart Update was processing events but `vk.com/klgdevents` got no new posts, and every nav-drain tick spammed superadmin with `Не удалось отредактировать пост https://vk.com/wall-231920894_981 … окно редактирования истекло`. Audit of yesterday's six VK commits (`4d8cc174`, `c18f76a5`, `bea7135d`, `3fcc2698`, `2909d95c`, `2024fa42`, `f04aa342`) showed `sync_vk_week_post` / `sync_vk_weekend_post` / `update_week_pages_for` were never touched — only the compact VK daily was rewritten, so the dead weekly/weekend wall-post publishers kept running.
  - **(a) vk_sync scheduling.** `schedule_event_update_tasks` previously skipped `vk_sync` whenever `event.source_vk_post_url` was non-empty. The 2026-05-17 import path also stores the external VK source URL there, so imported VK events never got a managed klgdevents post. The check now schedules `vk_sync` unless `source_vk_post_url` already points at the `VK_EVENTS_GROUP_ID` group (i.e. a klgdevents post we already published). Smart Update therefore publishes a klgdevents wall post for every imported event regardless of source (VK wall, Telegram, `/parse`). Confirmed via prod `ENQ [E5068] new` log — no `vk_sync:5068` was scheduled for the 2026-05-18 04:16 import of event `5068` despite the documented behavior. The standalone `/vk_auto_import` path (which produced `wall-231920894_1080` in yesterday's successful test) goes through its own publisher and was never affected.
  - **(b) VK weekly/weekend navigation retired end-to-end.** `JobTask.week_pages` is no longer enqueued anywhere (`schedule_event_update_tasks`, `ics_fix_nav`, `_drain_nav_tasks`, `publish_event_progress`). `update_week_pages_for`, `sync_vk_week_post`, `sync_vk_weekend_post` are stubbed no-ops kept only to drain pre-existing JobOutbox rows. `sync_weekend_page` no longer publishes to VK. Dead helpers removed: `build_week_vk_message`, `build_weekend_vk_message`, `_build_month_vk_nav_lines`, `_weekend_vk_lock`, `_week_vk_lock`, `_vk_week_next_run`, `_vk_weekend_next_run`, plus the unused `closest_week_page`/`weekend_count`/`cur_count`/`next_count` block inside `build_daily_sections_vk`. The handler binding `"week_pages": update_week_pages_for` stays registered for legacy queued jobs but is now a no-op; the enum and task labels remain for safe drain. This removes the recurring `edit_vk_post: skipping wall-231920894_{972,981,1020,…} post too old` warnings against the old klgdevents weekly/weekend posts and stops sending the matching superadmin notification, because the superadmin alert in `edit_vk_post` was restored and is now only triggered by legitimate paths (festival posts, manual edits).
  - **(c) Tests.** `tests/test_vk_week.py` deleted (covered only retired code). `tests/test_vk_weekend.py` now keeps only `test_sync_weekend_page_no_longer_publishes_vk` and `test_sync_vk_weekend_post_is_now_noop`, both asserting that `post_to_vk` / `edit_vk_post` are never called. `tests/test_vk_source.py` gets `test_managed_klgdevents_event_skips_vk_sync` and a stricter `test_vk_wall_source_still_gets_event_vk_sync` that sets both `source_post_url` and `source_vk_post_url` to the same external wall URL (the real production import shape), so the Bug 2 regression cannot resurface unnoticed.
- **CherryFlash / КОНБ prod launch (round-3 batch, 2026-05-17)**: shipped the КОНБ track in production mode for tomorrow's first prod run. (1) **Default publish mode flipped from `test` to `prod`** in `PARTNER_KONB_LIBRARY`. Operator can still force test mode with setting `partner_track_konb_publish_mode=test`. (2) **Prod story fanout is two independent best-effort targets** — `tg:@kaliningradlibrary:story` and `vk:konb39:story`, both `blocking=False, required=False`; success on one does not gate the other, and a failure on one does not fail the overall publish (operator brief: «вне зависимости от успеха публикации в какой-либо из них»). Round-1's `vk_story` `required=True` is removed. (3) **Schedule moved to `12:37 Europe/Kaliningrad`** — exactly 7 minutes after the eco/nature partner slot at 12:30; operator brief: «через 7 минут после запуска эко-природы». Updated in `PARTNER_TRACK_DEFAULT_TIMES["partner_konb_library_001"]`. (4) **Outro «при поддержке» gap tripled**: `extra_gap_before` on both the «при поддержке» row and the next «Полюбить» row raised from `1.0` → `2.0`, so the effective gap is now base + 2×base = 3×base on both sides of the caption (round-2 used 2× spacing — operator wanted more breathing room). (5) **Price line uses «руб.» instead of `₽`**: the CherryFlash title font (Bebas Neue) does not include the ruble codepoint and renders `.notdef` artefacts on paid events; `_format_display_price` now emits `N руб.` / `N–M руб.` / `ОТ N руб.` / `ДО M руб.`. Free events still emit `БЕСПЛАТНО`. Tests: 74 passed (`test_partner_tracks` updated to assert prod defaults + both prod targets non-required; `test_partner_track_scheduling` updated for 12:37). Docs: `docs/features/cherryflash/partner-story-tracks.md` КОНБ outro / selection-policy / schedule sections rewritten to the round-3 contract.
- **CherryFlash / КОНБ anti-repeat loosened (round-3 feedback, 2026-05-17)**: round-2 deploy left the КОНБ track failing with `CherryFlash popular review did not collect enough events (selected=0 min=1)` because the cooldown was effectively «no repeat unless ≥86400 sec since last show AND the event triggers a priority boost». A daily run that happens slightly earlier than yesterday's run left zero eligible events when the pool was small. Operator brief: «повторять анонс это не плохо, главное не в одном ролике и максимально не подряд». Fixes: (a) `_konb_event_priority` now sets `allow_repeat_after_days = 1` as the **default** for every КОНБ event (no longer gated on priority boosts), so all КОНБ events become repeat-eligible after one day. (b) `_priority_repeat_allowed` switches from the strict `≥86400 sec` check to a **calendar-day-boundary check in `Europe/Kaliningrad`** — an event shown yesterday-or-earlier is always allowed today, regardless of wall-clock alignment. (c) New `KONB_REPEAT_SCORE_PENALTY = 220.0` is subtracted from the score of every recently-shown КОНБ candidate (`_repeat_score_penalty`), so fresh events always sort ahead of repeats; a repeat only appears when the fresh pool can't fill `max_events`. Combined with the existing `_first_position_penalty` (~360 pts), a recently-slot-1 repeat pays ~580 pts and cannot pin to slot 1 the next day. Tests: 74 passed across `tests/test_partner_tracks.py`, `tests/test_cherryflash_full_render.py`, `tests/test_video_announce_popular_review.py`, `tests/test_partner_track_scheduling.py`, `tests/test_partner_track_isolation.py`. Docs: `docs/features/cherryflash/partner-story-tracks.md` КОНБ ranking-policy section rewritten to document the calendar-day-spaced repeat allowance and the fresh-first sort bias.
- **CherryFlash / КОНБ video announce — round-2 test-run feedback (2026-05-17)**: applied the operator's three-point review of the round-1 test render. (a) **Venue line collapsed into the bot's canonical single line** `ЛОКАЦИЯ • АДРЕС • ГОРОД` — the round-1 separate address row above the venue is removed; `_format_display_location` now accepts `address=…` and slots it between the location name and the city, falling back to `LOC • CITY` when the address is empty. The address rendering path in `_build_primary_blocks` and the `RenderScene.address_line` field were dropped. (b) **Outro is 30% smaller end-to-end** via the new `outro.global_scale` knob (`PARTNER_KONB_LIBRARY.outro["global_scale"] = 0.7`) — `_render_brand_outro_frame` multiplies stripe height, font size, and base gap by `global_scale`. Per-line `scale` ratios (1.0 / 0.42 / 0.8) are unchanged. (c) **«при поддержке» gets 2× empty space above and below**: new per-line `extra_gap_before` multiplier in the outro config — the «при поддержке» row sets `extra_gap_before=1.0` (gap above is doubled) and the next «Полюбить» row also sets it to `1.0` (gap below «при поддержке» is doubled too); the renderer adds the extra px before each stripe and includes them in the total-block-height centring math. (d) **3D-phone top-screen label both lines now use the SAME font** (`CYGRE_BOLD`, same size) — round-1 mixed `CYGRE_BOLD` large + `CYGRE_SEMIBOLD` small, which left «Калининградская област…» truncated on the second line; the new two-line top-label code in `make_screen_label_texture` picks the largest size that fits the wider of the two lines into the available width (range `s(58)…s(128)`), so the full «Калининградская областная / научная библиотека» reads as a single institution title at consistent size. The ambiguous «25%/30%» phrasing in the operator brief was resolved as 30% (the last-stated number); flag if the intended value was 25% — easy one-line fix in `partner_tracks.py`. Targeted tests pass (`45 passed` in `tests/test_partner_tracks.py` + `tests/test_cherryflash_full_render.py`). Docs: `docs/features/cherryflash/partner-story-tracks.md` КОНБ section updated with the round-2 intro/outro/scene-card contract.
- **CherryFlash / КОНБ video announce — round-1 test-run feedback (2026-05-17)**: applied the operator's 7-point review of the first `/v` → КОНБ run. (1) Intro overlay underline now spans below the actual last line of the kicker text (`make_overlay_texture` in [scripts/render_mobilefeed_intro_still.py](scripts/render_mobilefeed_intro_still.py)) — previously the stripe was hardcoded under the first line and so sat under `НАУЧНАЯ`, but the КОНБ kicker is `НАУЧНАЯ\nБИБЛИОТЕКА`, so the underline must follow `БИБЛИОТЕКА`. The new code iterates `kicker.splitlines()`, draws each line at its computed y, and places the stripe just below the bottom of the last line, width matching the last line. (2) `PARTNER_KONB_LIBRARY.intro_screen_top` (3D-phone screen top label) changed from `научная библиотека` to `Калининградская областная\nнаучная библиотека` so both lines render via the existing 2-line top-label path in `make_screen_label_texture`. (3) Each scene card now renders an explicit address line (e.g. `МИРА 9`) between the date and location lines, sourced from `event.location_address`. (4) Festival context still uses the existing `festival_line` slot in `_build_primary_blocks` (verified — no change needed; payload propagates `scene.festival` already). (5) КОНБ partner filter (`classify_event_konb_library` in [video_announce/partner_filters.py](video_announce/partner_filters.py)) no longer admits events purely on `Мира 9` address — the `мира 9` hint is removed from `KONB_LIBRARY_LOCATION_HINTS`, and a new `KONB_LIBRARY_EXCLUDED_LOCATION_HINTS` rejects any event whose `location_name`/title mentions `дом китобоя` / `музей китобоя`. Дом китобоя shares the building but is a different institution and must never pass through КОНБ ownership filtering. (6) Outro lines split into one stripe per word: «Калининградская» / «областная» / «научная» / «библиотека» at scale 1.0; «при поддержке» kept as a single small stripe (scale 0.42); «Полюбить» / «Калининград» / «Анонсы» at scale 0.8 (20% smaller than the library lines), per operator brief. (7) New free/price line rendered between the date and location lines in the same `DETAIL_COLOR` palette as the address: `БЕСПЛАТНО` when `is_free` (or both prices are absent/zero), otherwise the resolved `min ₽` / `min–max ₽` / `до max ₽` label from `ticket_price_min/max`. `build_payload` and the manifest `events` block now pass `location_address`, `is_free`, `ticket_price_min`, `ticket_price_max` to the renderer. Targeted tests: `tests/test_partner_tracks.py` (+ Дом-китобоя exclusion regressions), `tests/test_cherryflash_full_render.py`. Docs: [docs/features/cherryflash/partner-story-tracks.md](docs/features/cherryflash/partner-story-tracks.md) КОНБ section updated with the round-1 contract changes.
- **Fixed / CherryFlash КОНБ test publish**: fixed `partner_konb_library_001` test mode so `@keniggpt` receives the rendered video as a normal Telegram channel post, not as a channel story preflight that can fail with `BOOSTS_REQUIRED`. КОНБ test/prod modes now explicitly suppress inherited global Telegram Business fanout; only the configured КОНБ targets are present.
- **Fixed / CherryFlash east retry deferral**: the east-region `@natakkaz` watchdog now counts both new `skipped/missing_business_target` attempts and legacy `failed/did not create a session` attempts from the same local day, so already-running retry loops stop immediately after the retry budget is exceeded.
- **CherryFlash / КОНБ video announce**: added `partner_konb_library_001` (`popular_review_konb`) for Kaliningrad regional scientific library events. The first-run publish mode targets `@keniggpt`; production mode (`setting.partner_track_konb_publish_mode=prod`) publishes to `@kaliningradlibrary` Telegram Stories best-effort and `vk.com/konb39` VK Story as required. Selection has a КОНБ venue/source filter, all-future fallback when popularity hits are sparse, boosts for tickets/prices, explicit special guests, and 3-day/1-day pre-event exposure, plus first-slot anti-stickiness. Daily schedule defaults to `12:50 Europe/Kaliningrad`, just after the eco/nature partner slot. The custom outro uses `#780000`; the east-region `@natakkaz` partner track now stops same-day watchdog retries after the scheduled attempt plus one retry when the Business story target is still not connected.
- **VK publishing / announcement hashtags**: VK event posts in `klgdevents` and CherryFlash `vk_wall` video posts for `club231828790` now append announcement hashtags: base `#анонс #анонс39 #кудапойтиКалининград #афишаКалининград`, city tags for the selected/event cities, and both date forms (`#17мая` and `#17_мая`). CherryFlash derives the VK wall caption from the ready event selection rather than leaving the wall post at bare `Видеоанонс`.
- **VK publishing / plain headings**: VK event posts now sanitize Smart Update Markdown/HTML section headings before `wall.post`/`wall.edit`: unsupported `### Heading` text is rendered as an uppercase plain heading followed by a blank line, while event photos remain a single multi-photo attachment list so VK can render the media group as its native grid. Edits of managed event posts and photo uploads now go straight through the user-token actor when it is available, avoiding VK `code=27` group-auth retry loops; existing posts keep their current photo set if a re-upload is only partially successful during edit.
- **VK publishing / media, compact daily and CherryFlash fanout**: Smart Update event posts now target the dedicated VK events group (`VK_EVENTS_GROUP_ID`, defaulting to the historical afisha group), including events imported from VK wall posts; upload event media groups through the user-token actor instead of accidentally passing a group token to the photo upload flow; and default VK photo attachments to enabled unless `/vkphotos` explicitly disables them. VK daily is now compact and split by its existing slots: morning `НЕ ПРОПУСТИТЕ СЕГОДНЯ` and evening `N ДОБАВИЛИ В АНОНС`, with one-line event rows and links to event VK posts when available. CherryFlash now builds a mixed Telegram/VK story queue with 5-minute interleaving, publishes the video as a VK wall post plus VK stories for `club231828790` and `klgdevents`, passes `VK_ACCESS_TOKEN5` into encrypted Kaggle story secrets, and treats Telegram Business/story boost failures as target-local so VK and partner fanout can continue independently.
- **Incident / CherryFlash eco event-date recall**: opened `INC-2026-05-17-eco-cherryflash-underfilled-event-recall` after the 2026-05-17 `partner_eco_nature_001` story underfilled and missed same-day `Фестиваль «Зелёный Кёнигсберг»` because its source posts were from 2026-04-23, outside the 1/3/7-day popularity windows. The eco partner selector now adds bounded current/future event-date recall candidates (`source_window=partner_event_date_recall`) when the recent popularity pool underfills, while still requiring the existing LLM-first `eco_prirodnaya` partner filter before publication.
- **Guide Excursions / Source coverage**: added `@kaliningradlibrary` (`Калининградская областная научная библиотека`) to the canonical guide monitoring seed as an `organization_with_tours` source with `organization`, `mixed_topic`, and `library` flags, so library-led guided tours / walks / local-history excursions can enter the guide pipeline while ordinary library events remain LLM-screened noise.
- **Promo / `8 женщин` CherryFlash campaign and placement caps**: added production promo campaign `#2` for event `4617` (`Спектакль 8 ЖЕНЩИН`, show date 2026-05-22) ending 2026-05-21 with total exposure goal `2`, daily cap `1`, one `first_slot` CherryFlash exposure and one `guaranteed_any_position` CherryFlash exposure. The resolver now honors campaign/activity total goals, local-day caps, `slot=1` / `selection_policy=first_slot`, and allows an event campaign to end before the promoted event date. Deployed to Fly on 2026-05-17 as `bba67b5`.
- **Incident / CherryFlash eco promo leakage prevention**: extended `INC-2026-05-15-cherryflash-partner-fanout-promo-filter` after production sessions showed base `popular_review` promo candidates entering `popular_review_eco` without a bounded partner policy. The eco track now applies its partner `event_filter` to promo candidates; matching promo follows normal placement, while non-matching promo is limited to one item, only after at least three profile-matched eco/nature/local-history events are already available, and is downgraded to any-position placement. Other partner tracks still avoid base promo unless separately documented, and `manual_review` promo fails closed in automatic runs.
- **Incident / VK retrospective reschedule wrong postponement**: opened `INC-2026-05-17-vk-retrospective-reschedule-wrong-postponement` after production event `4617` (`8 женщин`) was wrongly marked `postponed` from an unrelated VK lecture post that said the current lecture was a replacement for a missed April meeting. Production data was repaired (`4617` restored active; bad `vk_cancel` source/fact/link removed; offending `vk_inbox` row marked skipped), and `vk_auto_queue` now keeps retrospective replacement wording on the normal LLM-first import path instead of the cancellation shortcut.
- **Incident / May 17 future event quality prevention**: opened `INC-2026-05-17-future-event-quality-regressions` after a fresh production DB snapshot found active future cards with person/prose venues (`Общая кухня`, `Закулисье театра`, `Сигнал`, `Открытое море`), section-label titles (`GROZA` / `неделя в театре`), date-as-time parsing (`17.05` -> `17:05`), and duplicate/split clusters. Telegram Monitoring is now hardened LLM-first for these shapes: service-heading title review covers theatre digest labels, venue review is triggered for obvious person names, server import drops person-name venues and date-marker times, unknown source defaults are split without fuzzy city-token remaps, and tests cover the Barn/GROZA regressions.
- **Incident / `kraftmarket39/235` TelegramMonitor false negative prevention**: opened `INC-2026-05-17-kraftmarket235-tg-monitoring-extraction-miss` after production evidence showed the Kaggle TelegramMonitor run scanned the clear `8 женщин` event post but returned `events=[]`, leaving no `event`, `event_source`, or `telegram_scanned_message` row. Producer extraction now has a narrow LLM single-event rescue for structurally clear dated/timed ticketed posts, and server import persists `producer_zero_events:clear_event_signals` diagnostics that are reprocessable when a later producer run returns valid events.
- **CherryFlash / festival context in video cards**: CherryFlash `payload.json` and `assets/cherryflash_selection.json` now preserve `event.festival`; the full-video renderer prints the festival name as its own line between title and date/location, so promoted festival items such as `80 историй о главном` remain visibly tied to the festival without forcing that context into the event title.
- **Incident / CherryFlash partner fanout, promo priority and eco fail-closed selection**: opened `INC-2026-05-15-cherryflash-partner-fanout-promo-filter` after the 2026-05-15 `partner_eco_nature_001` run published to shared story targets (`me`, `@kenigevents`, `@lovekenig`) in addition to the intended encrypted Business target and admitted unrelated eco candidates after `llm_error:ProviderError`. `story_publish` now treats explicit `story_targets_override=[]` as a real empty override, so partner tracks cannot inherit global Telethon channel fanout; partner auto-runs pass `admit_manual_review=False`; the eco classifier retries Gemma and then uses a `gpt-4o` fallback before failing closed. Promo campaigns now have `priority` (`0..3`, `0` highest), `/promo` and `/v` expose inline management buttons, `80 историй о главном` is normalized to priority `1`, and its video activity uses `guaranteed_any_position` so it is guaranteed into CherryFlash without forcing slot 1/2. Targeted checks: `47 passed` across partner tracks, story publish, promo, and popular-review tests.
- **Incident / INC-2026-05-16 Telegram location prose bypass + City Jazz fallback recurrence**: fixed two production-visible defect classes in `/digest` on 2026-05-16. (a) Prose-bypass: the server-side prose guard from [INC-2026-04-26](docs/reports/incidents/INC-2026-04-26-daily-location-fragments.md) dropped the LLM-supplied prose `location_name`, but `_infer_location_from_text` then re-picked the same prose as a venue candidate (because `_infer_city_from_location_string` treats any short non-numeric span like `"Это точка"` as a city, so the comma+city heuristic accepts `"Это точка, после которой ты уже не тот же."`). Added a final-stage prose gate in [source_parsing/telegram/handlers.py](source_parsing/telegram/handlers.py) that runs AFTER `normalise_event_location_from_reference` — only fires when normalisation did NOT resolve a canonical venue, so legitimate names containing prose-detector triggers (e.g. `Творческое пространство 12|55`) are not affected. Fixed events `4694` (`Шаманское путешествие` from `terkatalk/4818`) and `4990` (`Лабиринты Историй` from `terkatalk/4859`). (b) City Jazz fallback recurrence: added a known-venue grounding gate that drops an LLM-extracted location matching a canonical reference row when the venue is not mentioned in source text / poster OCR / `raw_excerpt`. Closes the class of `INC-2026-04-29-bar-bastion-city-jazz-location` for sources without a `default_location`. Fixed event `4991` (`Съёмки исторического кинопроекта` from `festdir/4357` — casting call with no venue in source, was assigned `Калининград Сити Джаз Клуб, Мира 33-35`). (c) `docs/features/telegram-monitoring/sources.yml`: seeded `default_location='Пространство Тёрка, Пл. Победы 4 (1 под. 2 этаж), Калининград'` for `terkatalk` — the channel belongs to that venue and previously had `default_location=NULL`, which made every venue-less post on this source a candidate for the prose fallback. (d) `docs/reference/locations.md`: added `СКЛАD, Ялтинская 20П, Калининград` (was missing for the `qtickets` Euphoria Party event `3801`); completed `Театральная гостиная Солёная ворона, Железнодорожная 1, Зеленоградск` (address was missing for VK source `194927034` Solenaya Vorona events including `4806`). (e) `docs/reference/location-aliases.md`: added aliases for both new/completed venues. New regression tests in [tests/test_tg_candidate_location_grounding.py](tests/test_tg_candidate_location_grounding.py): `test_tg_build_candidate_prose_inference_fallback_does_not_recreate_prose`, `test_tg_build_candidate_terkatalk_default_location_recovers_venue`, `test_tg_build_candidate_rejects_ungrounded_known_venue_from_extraction`.
- **CherryFlash / fanout: @LANGEANNA added**: extended the production main-track CherryFlash story fanout allowlist (`setting.video_announce_story_business_targets`) from `["@jane_tour39", "@Tatiana_K_39"]` to `["@jane_tour39", "@Tatiana_K_39", "@LANGEANNA"]`. The new target receives the same primary CherryFlash story as the other fanout accounts (no separate partner track / curation), through the existing `_business_story_targets` selector path. The partner must have granted the bot story-management rights once from their Telegram client; until the encrypted Business connection is cached, the selector silently skips the target.
- **Process / Requirements discipline rule**: added `Requirements Discipline` block to [CLAUDE.md](CLAUDE.md) plus a memory entry `feedback_requirement_discipline.md` after a round-4 user pushback on `afishathumb`. Rule: every refinement to a feature's requirements must land in the canonical doc (e.g. `docs/features/<name>/README.md`) the same turn it is stated by the user; contradictions with the original spec or with an earlier user statement must be surfaced (quote old + quote new + state intended interpretation) and confirmed BEFORE acting; each requirement change gets a CHANGELOG line. This applies even when the user says "just continue" — autonomy on execution does not extend to silently changing requirements. Without this rule the implementation kept drifting across 4 rounds of feedback on the same point (body material) because the previous correction was never recorded.
- **AfishaThumb round-8 placement discipline**: per a deeper operator review of round-7. (a) Title-banner heuristic refined — was "any multi-image event gets a banner" which duplicated the title on legitimate posters (4131 case); new rule: banner created ONLY when the LLM region detector reports `< 3` distinct info regions on the primary image (the signature of "this is a photo with one label" vs "this is a real event poster with title+date+location+price"). (b) Title sticker is a **BANNER** (wide aspect ≥ 3.5:1, dynamic sizing) placed ABOVE the primary image like a PowerPoint slide title — never inside the side-sticker stack (recorded as T1). (c) Round-7 multi-image cluster was stretching portrait primaries horizontally because `primary_h` was shrunk to make space for title + extras bands while `primary_w` stayed fixed — fixed by always computing primary at its aspect-correct dimensions and stacking the bands ABOVE / BELOW it (recorded as bug-of-round in the requirements ledger). (d) Round-7 extras strip was at `~26%` of primary's height — too small per operator's "max 1/2 of primary" rule (recorded as N1); now sized at exactly `50%` of the primary's longer side. (e) Side stickers' angular placement now anchors on the sticker's INNER edge instead of its centre, so wide stickers can never overlap the primary's arc regardless of width. (f) Strict paper-overlap validator added (round-8 T2) — every paper's (angular, z) bbox with tilt is intersection-tested against every other paper; collisions are printed as warnings so the operator can tighten placement. (g) New requirement ledger rows T1/T2/N1/N2/I4/F0 added to `docs/features/afishathumb/README.md`. F0 introduces a NEW phase before motion work: a 2D flat unrolled "Mercator" layout view of the planned cylinder surface showing every event's afishas + image cluster + all stickers + the camera path overlay, so the operator can approve the blueprint before the 3D render.
- **AfishaThumb round-7 multi-image cluster + camera grammar refinement**: per round-7 operator feedback. (a) Multi-image events (≥ 2 photos in `event.photo_urls`) now render as a real cluster: primary image keeps the prominent slot, up to 3 extras stack as a horizontal strip just below it; each extra is sized to fit its slot while preserving aspect. (b) When the event is multi-image, the `Title` sticker is always created and added to the cluster (because the LLM can mis-identify text on a sub-image as the event title — e.g. "Платакс Обыкновенный" on a fish photo inside an event titled "Музейная ночь"). (c) Per-paper `paper_offset` raised from `0.004` → `0.030+0.003 × layer_index` for stickers so heavily peeled poster corners (max lift `~0.019`) can no longer z-fight with sticker meshes (visible as "torn" vertical stripes in v5). (d) Cleanup list extends to `slot_wide.png` and `slot_close.png` to kill stale renders. (e) `render_date_card` font ratios: day `0.50 → 0.44`, month `0.16 → 0.24`, time `0.21 → 0.22` so «МАЯ»/«ИЮНЯ» reads at slot scale. (f) Cost sticker peel softened (`0.20–0.55 → 0.05–0.25`, peel-corner probability `0.30 → 0.12`) — the yellow «БЕСПЛАТНО» card no longer reads as crumpled. (g) Cylinder body voronoi tightened (`scale 7 → 22`, tone delta `±5%`) killing the "crocodile-skin patches" visible at distance. (h) Daylight lighting: sun `4.5 → 2.4`, fill `480 → 180`, bounce `220 → 110`, world `0.55 → 0.35`, exposure `-0.20`, AgX Medium-High Contrast — posters no longer read washed-out. (i) New helper `scripts/afishathumb/render_slot.sh` chains prepare → blender render → trace overlay so the camera trace is never out of sync with the latest render.
- **Docs / AfishaThumb camera grammar + LLM camera planner design**: added two new subsections to `docs/features/afishathumb/README.md`. "Camera grammar" specifies that the per-slot arc must combine multiple axes per beat — dolly+truck, crane/boom, tilt, angled (15-30° off-radial) approach, subtle dutch only on fast traversals — so the motion reads as a cinematographer's hand, not a turntable. "LLM-driven camera path planning" specifies the input contract (layout manifest + LLM region bboxes + event metadata + slot budget) and the output JSON schema for keypoints from `gemini-3.1-flash-lite`, with the deterministic planner staying in-loop as validator + fallback. Implementation deferred until per-slot visual layer is stable.
- **AfishaThumb Stage-2 v4 placement engine**: implemented the locked per-slot scene design end-to-end. New module `kaggle/AfishaThumb/scripts/poster_llm.py` calls `gemini-3.1-flash-lite` with the poster image and parses bbox coords back from Gemini's 0..1000 scale to 0..1, with 3 retries + on-disk cache + cv2-density fallback. `prepare_slot.py` now (a) skips date/location/cost/digest stickers when LLM confirms the info legibly on the poster (per-region area threshold 1.2%); (b) picks a per-event tightness budget in `[3°, 15°]` from a seeded RNG and uses it as the off-poster placement margin; (c) hard-rejects sticker candidates that overlap any LLM info region or any already-placed sticker; (d) prefers off-poster placement (−0.40 score) and falls back to poster edges before centre (−0.20 + edge bonus) when tightness is tight. `story_plan.py` rewritten under S1: 2 or 3 attention anchors (image + 1 or 2 key info focus pushes) instead of the previous 8-beat micro-stop chain; total slot budget ≈ 5.5 s with longer image dwell when only 2 anchors. Poster peel reduced under V3 to at most one strong + one optional diagonally-opposite mild corner with intensity 0.30–0.55. Cast iron body material rebuilt under V1 — no horizontal banding, uniform dark base + voronoi cells. First two test slots (event 4594 free landscape, event 4131 paid portrait) now read clean with 3 and 2 stickers respectively, both ≤ 5.5 s, all 6 coverage tests pass.
- **Docs / AfishaThumb per-slot algorithm locks**: C1 = sticker angular distance randomised per event in `[3°, 15°]` (operator confirmed `15°` cap with per-event variation, not a fixed value, so events don't look templated); S1 = 2 or 3 attention anchors per slot, chosen per event by seeded RNG `event_id` (operator left LLM-driven anchor count as a possible later variant once the deterministic path is stable); L1 = LLM region schema frozen at `title / date / time / location / price`, no extra fields; L3 = route-between-slots LLM optimisation deferred until per-slot algorithm is locked; Q6 = placement detail judgement reserved for generated examples rather than further doc-level iteration.
- **Docs / AfishaThumb V1 locked + per-slot scene design recorded**: V1 (body cylinder material) locked per operator confirmation 2026-05-15 — dark metal (cast iron or dark old steel), textured imperfect, may be black-painted on the poster-glue surface. Light cream/plaster interpretation from the LLM-generated spec block explicitly rejected. The supplementary LLM spec is kept in `requirements.md` (not deleted) but now formally classified as a hint that can have detail errors, with operator text + reference photo + chat refinements ranked above it. Added a new "Per-slot scene design (algorithm, awaiting operator review)" section to [docs/features/afishathumb/README.md](docs/features/afishathumb/README.md) covering: (a) inputs per event (DB row + poster image + LLM region detector + cv2 density fallback); (b) decision tree per info piece (poster vs sticker for each of image/title/essence/when/where/cost); (c) sticker placement algorithm with tight-cluster constraint `≤ 22°` off poster edge and LLM-region-avoiding scoring; (d) single-arc camera path (5.7 s) with 2 attention anchors (image + key-info) and all other info read drive-by; (e) six deterministic validation tests run before render. Multi-event route between slots deferred until per-slot algorithm is locked.
- **Docs / AfishaThumb source-of-truth fix + V1 unresolved**: corrected the canonical README header in [docs/features/afishathumb/README.md](docs/features/afishathumb/README.md) to explicitly flag that the `«Спецификация афишной тумбы для 3d»` block (lines 45+) in `afishathumb-requirements.md` is an auto-generated LLM supplement and **NOT** operator-authored — the actual canonical sources are (1) operator-authored text lines 1–44, (2) the reference photo `afishathumb-ref.png` (operator delegated geometry/material reconstruction to the photo on line 7), (3) `fon.png`, and (4) in-chat operator refinements. Hex codes from the LLM block (`#B0B5B0`, `#2A3B2C`, `#6C8E75`) are not requirements. V1 in the refined-requirements ledger is now marked **unresolved**: the reference photo shows a LIGHT weathered cylinder body (cream/whitish-grey), while operator chat rounds 1–4 called for DARK bare metal. Implementation paused on V1 pending operator pick between (A) follow photo, (B) follow chat, (C) reinterpret chat as referring to base/decoration only.
- **Docs / AfishaThumb refined requirements ledger**: opened a new "Refined requirements (feedback rounds 1–4)" section in [docs/features/afishathumb/README.md](docs/features/afishathumb/README.md) capturing all spec-vs-implementation deltas accumulated during Stage-2 iteration. Notable: V3 (peel must not hurt readability); C1 (cluster TIGHT around poster, ≤ ~25° off poster edge); S1 (camera path = 1–3 smooth arcs, NOT 8 micro-stops); S2 (inter-slot transitions = slide-with-mild-backout, not full pull-out); L1 (Gemini-lite poster region detection with cv2 density fallback) and L3 (LLM route optimisation deferred for decision after L1 lands).
- **Docs / AfishaThumb canonical scaffold**: opened canonical [docs/features/afishathumb/README.md](docs/features/afishathumb/README.md) for the new third video-announce product (3D advertising-column scene rendered entirely in Blender). Codifies product identity (`profile_key=afishathumb`, planned Kaggle slug `zigomaro/afishathumb`), the visual contract (column geometry per [requirements](docs/backlog/features/afishathumb/afishathumb-requirements.md), poster + info-sticker grammar, image-only posters with title moved to neighbour sticker, multi-image clusters up to 4 per event, near-flat crumple reuse from `CrumpleVideo`, promo "обратите внимание" stickers with `≤2` anchors / not first / never two-in-a-row), camera language (continuous motion, eased moves, image→info focus order, pre-computed path), selection contract (CherryFlash `popular_review` extended with up to 6 main slots + excursions + `search_digest` fallback), outro (two stickers «Полюбить Калининград Анонсы» + «Мост в Кёнигсберг» glued on the same cylinder), publication boundary (test `@keniggpt`, prod story `@mostvkenig`, no channel-chain fanout at MVP), and the four-stage rollout (selection probe → per-slot stills → fast camera pass → Kaggle HQ). Wired into [docs/routes.yml](docs/routes.yml) under `backlog.features.afishathumb` together with the requirements/reference/background asset paths. No code yet; selection + Blender scene + Kaggle runtime live as open implementation items.
- **Promo / CherryFlash reporting + slot order**: scheduled viewer-facing CherryFlash publications are now counted and reported as promo shows even when the legacy video session status is stored as `PUBLISHED_TEST`; `/promo report` shows per-session publication details, and general promo items are interleaved with organic picks so the first promo usually lands in slot 1 or 2 without taking both leading slots.
- **Promo Campaigns MVP / 80 Stories CherryFlash boost**: added the `promo_campaign` / `promo_target` / `promo_activity` / `promo_exposure` schema, `/promo` superadmin command, `/a` promo routing, flexible event/festival campaign creation, and the initial `80 историй о главном` campaign seed through 2026-07-18. CherryFlash now injects up to two eligible future promo events into organic popularity picks, stores promo provenance on `videoannounce_item`, rotates festival rows by viewer-facing exposure count, and daily "добавили в анонс" rows can mark promoted events with `✨` without printing `promo`.
- **CherryFlash / Partner tracks daily schedule + Kenigsberg slot shift to 19:30**: both partner tracks now publish automatically every day at fixed local Europe/Kaliningrad slots — `partner_eco_nature_001` at `12:30` and `partner_region_east_001` at `18:30` — through new APScheduler cron jobs `video_partner_track_eco` / `video_partner_track_east` driven by `_run_scheduled_partner_track`. Each track ships with a `*_watchdog` interval job running every 10 minutes that detects a missed slot (no confirmed Kaggle handoff for today's `target_date` under the track's `profile_key`) and re-launches the partner pipeline; retries stop at the hard local deadline `22:00` so nothing publishes overnight. Anti-repeat scoping by `profile_key=popular_review_<track>` keeps re-runs idempotent. Slot picks come from log-based analysis of existing heavy jobs (`source_parsing_day` 14:15, `video_tomorrow` 16:45, `guide_excursions_full` 20:10, plus base CherryFlash 10:15): the eco window has ~100 min of clean runway before 14:15 even on CPU; the east window has ~100 min of runway between `video_tomorrow` finish and the next heavy peak. To clear that next heavy peak `kenigsberg_story_daily` was moved from `20:10` to `19:30 Europe/Kaliningrad` (the previous slot was a daily double-peak with `guide_excursions_full`). Telegram-session impact: partner tracks publish only through Bot API `postStory` to encrypted Business targets and do not load `TELEGRAM_AUTH_BUNDLE_S22` at all, so there is no Telethon-session contention with base CherryFlash's channel chain or Kenigsberg's primary upload — a second Telegram session is not required. No feature flags: per user direction `ENABLE_*` flags are intentionally absent so the schedule cannot silently regress to off; only per-track time overrides `V_PARTNER_TRACK_ECO_TIME_LOCAL` / `V_PARTNER_TRACK_EAST_TIME_LOCAL` are env-tunable without redeploy. New: `_run_scheduled_partner_track`, `_partner_track_session_exists_today`, `maybe_dispatch_partner_track_watchdog`, `PARTNER_TRACK_TZ`/`PARTNER_TRACK_DEFAULT_TIMES`/`PARTNER_TRACK_RETRY_DEADLINE_LOCAL` in `scheduling.py`; `tests/test_partner_track_scheduling.py` covers dispatch on missed slot, skip when today's session already remote-handed-off, hard `22:00` deadline, before-slot silence, profile-key scoping (base CherryFlash success does not satisfy eco watchdog) and env override.
- **CherryFlash / Partner Story Tracks MVP (`partner_eco_nature_001` + `partner_region_east_001`)**: shipped the first two partner-specific CherryFlash story tracks end-to-end. `/v` now exposes one-click launches for `🍃 Природа и экология` and `🌾 Восток области`, each running through a dedicated `run_partner_track_pipeline()` that wires per-track `partner_track_id`/`profile_key`/`content_filter_id`/`geo_filter_id` into `selection_params` and publishes only to the per-track encrypted Telegram Business target (no `@kenigevents`/`@lovekenig` channel-chain fanout). Selection sits on top of the existing CherryFlash popularity pool: `kaliningrad_region_east` is a deterministic `exclude_over_include` geo gate keyed on `event.city`/`location_*` with included/excluded settlement lists from the spec, and `eco_prirodnaya` is an LLM-first Gemma 4 classifier (`models/gemma-4-31b-it`) returning native-schema `decision ∈ {matched, manual_review, exclude}` plus a keyword-hint pre-pass — `manual_review` is admitted with a warning so the partner universe does not starve. Anti-repeat (`POPULAR_REVIEW_ANTI_REPEAT_DAYS`) now scopes by partner `profile_key`. Partner targets resolve through new `Setting` rows `partner_track_eco_business_selector` / `partner_track_east_business_selector` (operator-supplied via `/check_business` selector value, e.g. a `@username` matched against the cached `username_hash`); raw handles never enter repo files. Parallel safety: the existing `has_rendering()` guard already serializes any RENDERING session, so partner tracks are mutually serialized with base CherryFlash without DB-schema changes. New: `video_announce/partner_tracks.py` registry, `video_announce/partner_filters.py` filters + Gemma 4 transport adapter, `tests/test_partner_tracks.py` covering registry + east deterministic + eco LLM-first contract.
- **CherryFlash / Per-track intro labels (`Природа и экология`, `Восток Калининградской области`)**: replaced the hardcoded `ПОПУЛЯРНОЕ` 2D-phone kicker and `популярное сейчас` 3D-phone screen-top with manifest-driven overrides. `scripts/render_mobilefeed_intro_still.py::_default_variants` accepts a new `kicker` parameter and `_load_runtime_selection` reads `variant.kicker`/`variant.screen_top` from `selection_manifest.json`. `video_announce/scenario.py::_build_cherryflash_selection_manifest` emits these overrides (plus the partner `selection_profile_key` and `partner_track_id`) from the new `selection_params["variant_overrides"]` bag; per-track values live in `PartnerTrack.intro_kicker`/`intro_screen_top` so future tracks plug in a one-line config.
- **CherryFlash / "Удалить неудачную партнёрскую публикацию" button**: `/v` now has a `🗑 Удалить неудачную партнёрскую публикацию` entry that opens a per-track menu, shows the last persisted story (id + publish time) per partner, asks for explicit confirmation, then invokes a new `_delete_business_story()` helper in `main.py` against Bot API `deleteStory(business_connection_id, story_id)`. Deletion is partner-only (no channel-chain reversal — partner tracks have no chain). Persisted on `VideoAnnounceSession` via new columns `partner_track_id` / `partner_story_id` / `partner_story_connection_hash` / `partner_story_deleted_at` (Alembic migration `20260514_partner_story_metadata` + `db.py` startup shim for SQLite ALTER), captured at poller-time from the existing `story_publish_report.json` by new `_extract_partner_story_metadata` / `_persist_partner_story_metadata` (it filters `transport=telegram_business` ok-targets with non-empty `story_id`).
- **CherryFlash / Leading-emoji title rendering fix**: event titles like `🎸 Концерт …` rendered as `.notdef` (unknown-glyph box) in the CherryFlash video because Bebas Neue has no emoji coverage and the rasterizer (`PIL.ImageFont.truetype`) has no fallback chain. `scripts/render_cherryflash_full.py::_build_render_scenes` now strips the leading emoji + whitespace via `_strip_leading_emoji()` (covers symbols/pictographs/emoticons/dingbats/regional-indicator-flags + ZWJ/variation selectors) before `RenderScene.title` is built; inline emoji inside the title are intentionally preserved (only the leading run is stripped). New `tests/test_cherryflash_emoji_strip.py` covers the parametrized cases (`moviepy`-gated).
- **Kenigsberg Stories / channel-first publish with `me` fallback + extra forwards**: `/kenigsberg` (and the scheduled `kenigsberg_story_daily`) now publishes the production story as the channel `@mostvkenig` so reposts attribute to the channel rather than the personal account, with the shared Kaggle helper transparently falling back to `peer=me` when channel-native story rights are still unavailable (e.g. `BOOSTS_REQUIRED`). After the primary story is live, the helper additionally reposts (`repost_previous` forward) it to `@loving_guide39` and `@jane_tour39` with the standard `600` second spacing, mirroring CherryFlash's fanout pattern. The contract is implemented through a new `fallback_peer` field on story targets in `kaggle/CrumpleVideo/story_publish.py` and `video_announce/story_publish.py::StoryTarget`, plumbed through `_parse_selection_targets` / `_parse_targets_json_env` and consumed by the shared `_story_targets_report` runtime. Canonical doc `docs/features/kenigsberg-stories/README.md` updated; new test coverage in `tests/test_kaggle_story_publish.py` (fallback success + primary success cases) and updated assertions in `tests/test_kenigsberg_stories.py::test_kenigsberg_production_story_config_uses_mostvkenig_and_native_profile`.
- **Incident / Kenigsberg wrong story fanout + Promise vocalise**: stopped Kenigsberg from inheriting the shared video-announcement Business story allowlist. Production story config now uploads to the configured Telethon account as the required target and attempts `@mostvkenig` only as best-effort `repost_previous`, with `story_business_targets=[]`. The mistaken Business stories from compensation issue `#44` were deleted through Bot API, the problematic first `The Promise` range (`3:44-4:26`) was removed, and music selection now prefers low-voice non-overlapping candidates before high-voice fresh-track fallback.
- **Incident / Kenigsberg production story preflight**: fixed the first production catch-up failure where direct Telethon story upload to `@mostvkenig` returned Telegram `BOOSTS_REQUIRED` and blocked the render before the fallback story path could publish.
- **Kenigsberg Stories / production schedule**: promoted Kenigsberg Stories to production publishing without Kenigsberg-specific launch/production ENV gates. Manual and scheduled runs now build the production story config for `@mostvkenig`; APScheduler runs `kenigsberg_story_daily` every day at `20:10 Europe/Kaliningrad` with startup catch-up after a missed same-day slot. Dataset period selection is weighted by mounted video-file counts, and production recency windows can be reset through `recent_usage_reset_at` while preserving hard `/a` source bans and issue history.
- **Kenigsberg Stories / no text truncation + stronger music selector**: removed the renderer's silent four-stripe cap that dropped tails such as `«приданое для невест»`; stripe wrapping now keeps the full text and allows up to seven rows on the vertical canvas before failing. Music selection now uses tiered pools and same-track fatigue instead of letting low raw `voice_risk` repeatedly pick recently used windows like `The Promise`; optional `music_ranges.json` / `kenigsberg_music_ranges.json` in the Kaggle music dataset can add whitelisted ranges for newly uploaded tracks.
- **Kenigsberg Stories / anti-repeat levels**: separated anti-repeat semantics for source footage. Operator `/a` bans stay hard, recent issue history is a soft distribution preference, and current-issue source overlap is strict with only an emergency fallback when no non-overlapping segment exists. Renderer manifests now distinguish `fresh`, `recent_soft_fallback`, and `current_emergency_fallback` selection modes.
- **Incident / Kenigsberg soft source exclusions**: fixed session `#279` failure where recent source exclusions and intra-run anti-repeat were treated as hard bans and the winter dataset could fail with `No source segment can avoid bans for slot 2`. User `/a` source bans remain hard, while `reason=recent_generation` and current-generation anti-repeat are now soft preferences with manifest/log flags when the renderer must fall back.
- **Incident / Kenigsberg repeated vocalized music windows**: Kenigsberg now passes recent music windows from issue manifests into Kaggle, records new music usage back into `kenigsberg_stories_state`, and scores candidate audio windows to avoid immediate same-track/overlapping repeats. The renderer also adds a best-effort `voice_risk` penalty for whitelisted fragments with vocalized audio and writes the score/repeat flags into manifest and render logs. The curated Kenigsberg thoughts file was expanded with the latest operator-provided entries.
- **Incident / Kenigsberg concurrent render gate**: replaced the global SQLite `RENDERING` uniqueness guard on `videoannounce_session.status` with a profile-scoped guard (`COALESCE(profile_key, 'default')`) so Kenigsberg can launch while an unrelated CrumpleVideo/default render is running, while still preventing two simultaneous Kenigsberg renders.
- **Incident / Kenigsberg text split 4o fallback**: kept Gemini lite as the primary semantic screen-split model for curated `thoughts.md` entries, but added an explicit validated `gpt-4o` fallback through `ask_4o` when Gemini lite fails provider checks or returns an invalid split. The path still disables shared Google/Gemma fallback and still fails before Kaggle if both models cannot preserve the source text exactly.
- **Kenigsberg Stories / rhythm resilience**: adjusted the beat-synced timing upgrade so strong-beat endings are preferred when the detected grid reaches a normal story length, but short/failed beat analysis falls back to an explicit target-duration timing path instead of publishing sharply shortened clips or blocking the render.
- **Kenigsberg Stories / rhythm audit and outro boundary**: tightened beat-synced montage construction so the main video now ends on a detected strong beat before the outro instead of padding the last scene to an arbitrary `18.0s` boundary. The manifest/render log now include raw detected `beat_times` and selected `strong_beat_times` so future issues can be audited against the actual selected music segment.
- **Incident / Kenigsberg text split fallback noise**: disabled the shared `GOOGLE_AI_FALLBACK_MODELS` chain and Telegram LLM incident notifications for Kenigsberg's text-screen split. The step now retries only the configured Gemini-lite model with Kenigsberg-specific delays (`KENIGSBERG_STORIES_TEXT_SPLIT_RETRY_DELAYS_SEC`) and still fails before Kaggle if all attempts fail or the split changes the curated `thoughts.md` text.
- **Incident / Kenigsberg launch command silence**: hardened the manual `/kenigsberg` command so it acknowledges immediately before admin/DB preflight and moves the long Kenigsberg preflight/Kaggle handoff into a background task. This keeps the Telegram update from sitting inside DB/Kaggle/LLM-adjacent work, reports a clear message if a previous Kenigsberg handoff is already in progress, and preserves the existing `profile_key="kenigsberg_story"` active-session gate so CherryFlash/CrumpleVideo/default video sessions do not block a Kenigsberg manual launch.
- **Incident / Kenigsberg LLM-only text split + beat-synced cuts**: fixed Kenigsberg `#13` text truncation and rhythm contract regressions. Story text now calls `gemini-3.1-flash-lite` only to choose semantic screen boundaries for the curated `thoughts.md` entry; validation fails before Kaggle if the LLM changes punctuation/words, drops the tail, returns overlong screens, or is unavailable after bounded retries. Kaggle no longer splits long text locally and rejects overlong unsplit `scene_lines`. Scene timing now uses `librosa` beat/onset analysis of the selected audio: first slot may be partial to the first strong beat, then cuts use only random `1x`/`2x` strong-beat spans; fake random rhythm grids are forbidden. The renderer also masks the bottom source strip (`KENIGSBERG_STORIES_BOTTOM_MASK_PX`, default `34`) so thin grey/light-text artifacts at the lower edge do not appear in stories.
- **Docs / Promo campaigns design**: added the canonical backlog spec for campaign-based event/festival promotion across `/promo`, `/a`, video announcements, daily announcements, and Telegraph month/weekend pages. The design includes campaign/activity/exposure data model, subtle `✨` highlighting for "добавили в анонс" without printing `promo`, CherryFlash/CrumpleVideo placement rules, reporting requirements, the initial `80 историй о главном` test campaign through 2026-07-18, and the `INC-2026-05-05-80-stories-video-promo-gap` regression checks.
- **Incident / Kenigsberg story-helper import regression**: fixed session `#267` failure in `zigomaro/koenigsberg-stories` where the notebook imported the shared story helper during a normal test render without `story_publish.json`; Kaggle did not have `telethon`, so rendering failed before `render_kenigsberg_story.py` started. The notebook now checks `story_publish_requested` first and imports/preflights the helper only when story publishing is actually requested; it also installs `telethon`, `requests`, and `cryptography` for the future production-story path. Failed renders no longer consume the selected thought from the shuffle-bag: `used_thought_ids` is updated only after a successful issue manifest is registered.
- **LLM / Guide Excursions Monitoring narrow Gemini-lite migration**: applied the same narrow split that Smart Update used on `facts_extract` + writer to the guide-excursions monitoring pipeline. Two stages are now routed to `gemini-3.1-flash-lite` by default with transparent fallback to `gemma-4-31b-it` through `GOOGLE_AI_FALLBACK_MODELS`: (a) Kaggle-side Tier-1 announce extraction `trail_scout.announce_extract_tier1.v1` and its `tier1_extract_block` rescue variant (consumers `guide_scout_announce_tier1_extract` / `guide_scout_tier1_extract_block`) via new `DEFAULT_GUIDE_MONITORING_EXTRACT_MODEL` in [kaggle/GuideExcursionsMonitor/guide_excursions_monitor.py](kaggle/GuideExcursionsMonitor/guide_excursions_monitor.py) and the env override `GUIDE_MONITORING_EXTRACT_MODEL`; (b) server-side `guide_excursions_digest_batch` Lollipop Trails writer in [guide_excursions/digest_writer.py](guide_excursions/digest_writer.py) via env override `GUIDE_DIGEST_WRITER_MODEL`. Motivation: extraction completeness is a known bottleneck (Gemma 4 `GE-EVAL` final `6/7` pass; shared miss `GE-EVAL-02` is the announce-in-reportage-tail class where Smart Update Lite shines), and digest writer copy is the daily user-facing surface — the same pair Smart Update moved on 2026-05-09 with a clear quality lift. Stages intentionally **not** moved: `trail_scout.screen.v1` (highest volume, Gemma 4 canary `2026-04-20` clean), `trail_scout.status_claim_extract.v1`, `trail_scout.template_extract.v1`, `route_weaver.enrich.v1`, server-side `guide_occurrence_enrich` (both `main_hook` and `audience_region_fit` batches), `guide_profile_enrich`, `guide_excursions_dedup` pair-judge, and `guide_scout_ocr` — they stay on Gemma 4 so the Lite RPD budget is not burned on classifier-style or multimodal work. Per-key budget check: under current load (3 scheduled runs/day = 1 full + 2 light × 10 sources) the Lite spend is `~25-35` requests/day vs the `500 RPD` cap on `GOOGLE_API_KEY2` (~5-7% of budget); the same key services Smart Update via `GOOGLE_API_KEY`, not `GOOGLE_API_KEY2`, so no cross-feature contention. Rollback without redeploy: set `GUIDE_MONITORING_EXTRACT_MODEL=models/gemma-4-31b-it` and `GUIDE_DIGEST_WRITER_MODEL=gemma-4-31b`. Docs updated in [docs/features/guide-excursions-monitoring/README.md](docs/features/guide-excursions-monitoring/README.md) (model split section + `Что уже мигрировано` migration `2026-05-12` line).
- **Kenigsberg Stories / intra-run anti-repeat and production story readiness**: fixed the renderer so one generated story keeps an in-memory source-range exclusion map and cannot reuse an overlapping or near-adjacent interval from the same source video later in the same issue; the rhythm grid and payload seed are now written to manifest/log evidence for cadence audits. Kenigsberg also now bundles the shared CherryFlash/Crumple story helper and can generate per-run encrypted story publish config for `@mostvkenig` only when `KENIGSBERG_STORIES_PRODUCTION_ENABLED=1`; production story publishing and scheduling remain disabled until the explicit production command.
- **Incident / Kenigsberg local handoff lock**: extended `INC-2026-05-12-kenigsberg-postprocess-db-lock-and-final-copy.md` after session `#266` created and bound `zigomaro/kenigsberg-session-266-1778610442` on Kaggle but failed to persist `kaggle_dataset`/`kaggle_kernel_ref` because SQLite was locked, leaving the row as `RENDERING + local:KoenigsbergStories`. Kenigsberg launch handoff writes now retry SQLite locks, stale `local:*` pre-handoff rows auto-fail on the next `/kenigsberg`, and `/kenigsberg unlock` can manually fail a stuck local handoff so a new run can start.
- **Incident / Kenigsberg post-processing lock + final copy source**: opened `INC-2026-05-12-kenigsberg-postprocess-db-lock-and-final-copy.md` after session `#265` had reached Kaggle `COMPLETE` but `/kenigsberg` still answered with a generic rendering lock while local download/publish/status work was delayed by SQLite contention from VK auto-import. Kenigsberg active-session checks now scope to `profile_key="kenigsberg_story"` and report Kaggle `COMPLETE` as finalization/download/publish, video session status updates and Kenigsberg state writes retry transient SQLite locks, `/a kenigsberg покажи список банов` canonicalizes to `/kenigsberg bans`, story text is now taken from curated `docs/features/kenigsberg-stories/thoughts.md` as final copy with only non-rewriting screen splitting (`text_source=thoughts_md`), and source scenes are decoded through ffmpeg CFR `30fps` extraction before overlays to avoid visible freezes from repeating the last frame of low/VFR-fps source clips.
- **Incident / Kenigsberg `/a` ban command routing and range precision**: opened `INC-2026-05-12-kenigsberg-assist-ban-routing-and-dominant-range.md` after `/a Kenigsberg #4 бан 4-6` executed `/kenigsberg #4 бан 4-6`, which the command rejected, leaving bans empty. `/a` and direct `/kenigsberg` now canonicalize reordered ban args to `/kenigsberg ban #N ranges`; timeline bans now map each whole-second operator range to one dominant source segment by maximum overlap instead of banning every edge-overlapped scene.
- **Incident / Kenigsberg deterministic text fallback quality**: opened `INC-2026-05-12-kenigsberg-deterministic-text-fallback-quality.md` after issue `#3` launched with `text=fallback` and produced visibly broken semantic cuts. The initial mitigation removed Gemma 4 from the text path and disabled Kaggle-side deterministic splitting; the current publication contract is superseded by the final-copy `thoughts.md` path above.
- **Incident / Kenigsberg music range overrun into vocals**: opened `INC-2026-05-12-kenigsberg-music-range-overrun-into-vocals` after test publication `https://t.me/keniggpt/1944` used audio outside the configured instrumental whitelist. Root cause: the renderer validated only the 18-second main montage against the allowed range, then encoded the full story including outro. Music selection now requires the full encoded duration to fit inside the allowed range, skips unlisted tracks instead of falling back to whole-track audio, matches track names more robustly, and records selected start/end plus allowed range in the manifest/render log.
