- **Incident / Kenigsberg command silent during Gemma retry**: opened `INC-2026-05-12-kenigsberg-command-silent-during-gemma-retry` after production `/kenigsberg` appeared silent for 142 seconds while the pre-launch Gemma 4 rewrite retried a provider `503 UNAVAILABLE`. The command now sends an immediate operator acknowledgement after reserving the issue/thought; the follow-up text-quality incident moved rewrite to `gemini-3.1-flash-lite` and fail-closed semantics instead of deterministic story text.
- **Kenigsberg Stories / animation and local Kaggle smoke polish**: aligned the Kenigsberg outro with CherryFlash `brand_outro` mechanics (black background, yellow strip blocks, condensed uppercase type, alternating slide-in with exponential ease) while keeping the required two screens; added a separate Gemma 4 rewrite step for `hook` + `scene_lines` before Kaggle launch, decoupled text cue timing from video segment timing, delayed text reveal until the stripe is substantially visible, moved scene text lower, switched stripe reveal to eased/back inertia, extended hold time for short text, added real two-frame scene crossfades, stopped repeating source frames on decode fallback, seeded renders from `secrets.randbits()` + `time_ns()` instead of predictable issue/time seconds, randomized the fallback rhythm grid more broadly, sent recent source segments from issue manifests into the next run as temporary source exclusions, copied `thoughts.md` into each session bundle for auditability, added `kenigsberg_render_log.json` with mounted video samples, and deleted the full frame sequence after encoding so Kaggle output now downloads the final MP4, manifests/log JSON and three preview frames instead of hundreds of JPEGs. Local Kaggle smokes through `zigomaro/kenigsberg-session-local-1778585647` completed with downloadable `kenigsberg_story_final.mp4` outputs under 4 MB.
- **Incident / Kenigsberg video dataset mount discovery**: updated `INC-2026-05-12-kenigsberg-winter-dataset-not-mounted` after the third production `/kenigsberg` smoke showed Kaggle mounting static inputs under `/kaggle/input/datasets` rather than top-level dataset directories. Removed server-side period/dataset selection and the `KENIGSBERG_STORIES_PERIODS` env switch entirely; the Kaggle renderer now recursively discovers mounted video datasets, randomly chooses among the available periods inside Kaggle, handles nested Kaggle layouts, and reports mounted input directories when video/music discovery fails. Tests now pin nested dataset discovery and random selection across mounted datasets.
- **Incident / Kenigsberg notebook escaped newlines**: opened `INC-2026-05-12-kenigsberg-notebook-escaped-newlines` after the first production `/kenigsberg` smoke created session `#253` but Kaggle failed in `zigomaro/koenigsberg-stories` cell 1 with `SyntaxError: unexpected character after line continuation character`; root cause was literal `\\n` sequences in `kaggle/KoenigsbergStories/koenigsberg_stories.ipynb` code-cell source. Fixed the notebook to use real line breaks, bumped the notebook version to `v2-mvp-heuristic-render`, and added `tests/test_kenigsberg_notebook.py` to parse the notebook, reject literal `\\n` in joined code cells, and compile each code cell before deploy.
- **Kenigsberg Stories / manual Kaggle MVP render**: wired `/kenigsberg` to launch a real manual Kaggle render when `KENIGSBERG_STORIES_KAGGLE_ENABLED=1` (enabled in `fly.toml` for manual-only testing). The command now creates a `kenigsberg_story` `VideoAnnounceSession`, selects a thought from `docs/features/kenigsberg-stories/thoughts.md`, reserves an issue number, creates a per-run `kenigsberg-session-*` Kaggle dataset, deploys the new local kernel `kaggle/KoenigsbergStories`, and lets the existing video poller post the generated MP4 to `@keniggpt`; period selection belongs to the Kaggle renderer, which chooses from mounted video datasets. Added `scripts/render_kenigsberg_story.py`, a first `heuristic_v1` renderer using source video datasets, `zigomaro/koenigsberg-music`, bottom crop for VEO, stripe typography, watermark, two-screen outro, H.265 story-safe encoding, and `kenigsberg_issue_manifest.json`; the poller imports that manifest back into `setting.kenigsberg_stories_state` so `/a`/`/kenigsberg ban` can map generated seconds to source-video bans after a successful run.
- **Kenigsberg Stories / safe command scaffold + `/a` bans**: added the superadmin-only `/kenigsberg` command surface for the upcoming `Мост в Кёнигсберг` story generator with launch gated by `KENIGSBERG_STORIES_KAGGLE_ENABLED`, plus `status`, `bans`, `ban #N ranges`, and `bans reset` subcommands. Added MVP state helpers in `kenigsberg_stories.state` that parse generated-video second ranges and map them back to source-video coordinates from persisted issue manifests before storing source bans in `setting.kenigsberg_stories_state`. `/a` now has an allowlisted `kenigsberg` action and a deterministic heuristic for requests like `в выпуске kenigsberg #15 бан 1-3, 7, 16-17`, routing them to `/kenigsberg ban #15 1-3, 7, 16-17` after confirmation. Canonical docs now spell out the `/a` control layer and explicitly defer full Gemma 4 visual clip judging to `gemma4_clip_judge_v2`, keeping MVP cuts on logged `heuristic_v1`.
- **Docs / Kenigsberg Stories MVP spec**: added the canonical feature draft for the Kaggle-based `Мост в Кёнигсберг` Telegram Story generator under `docs/features/kenigsberg-stories/README.md`, including CherryFlash reuse boundaries, beat-driven montage contract, Telegram Business story publishing boundary, `/kenigsberg` and `/a` command expectations, scheduling risks, and open questions. Added the initial wisdom-thought pool in `docs/features/kenigsberg-stories/thoughts.md` and wired the feature into `docs/features/README.md` plus `docs/routes.yml`.
- **Incident / zoo lecture premium-emoji `is_free` lost + lecture bullet blocks truncated**: opened `INC-2026-05-11-zoo-lecture-premium-emoji-and-bullet-block-truncation` after operator review of production event 4798 («Зоопарку — быть!», 2026-06-26 18:30, source `t.me/kraftmarket39/…`) showed two visible defects: `is_free=false` even though the post carried four premium `🆓🆓🆓🆓` custom-emoji marking free attendance, and the lecture content blocks «О чём поговорим» (3 bullets) and «Правда ли, что» (2 bullets) survived as one bullet each in `event_source_fact`, leaving a stub description. Two-stage LLM-first fix: (a) `kaggle/TelegramMonitor/telegram_monitor.py::strip_custom_emoji_entities` no longer replaces every custom-emoji range with spaces — it now consults a new helper `_custom_emoji_fallback_is_meaningful` that classifies the Unicode fallback by codepoint block (Pictographs `U+1F300..U+1FAFF`, Enclosed Alphanumerics `U+1F100..U+1F1FF`, Misc Symbols / Dingbats `U+2600..U+27BF`, Misc Technical `U+2300..U+23FF`, Geometric Shapes `U+25A0..U+25FF`); real pictographs are preserved so `🆓` reaches the LLM, PUA / non-pictograph placeholders are still replaced. Mirrored into `kaggle/TelegramMonitor/telegram_monitor.ipynb` cell 1 — the Kaggle kernel deploy carries the change to prod. (b) `smart_event_update.py::rich_facts_extract` `program_or_examples` rule now enumerates the common Russian lecture bullet-block headers (`О чём поговорим`, `Правда ли, что`, `Темы`, `Вопросы`, `В программе`, `Что обсудим`, `План встречи`) and explicitly forbids collapsing a 3-bullet block into one summary fact (`Если в источнике 3 bullet'а под "О чём поговорим" — верни 3 факта`). Three new tests in `tests/test_tg_monitor_gemma4_contract.py` pin the strip behaviour (static + functional via `exec`), one new test in `tests/test_smart_update_native_schema.py` pins the bullet-preservation rule against the real 4798 source excerpt. After both the Kaggle kernel re-deploy and the Fly main deploy, event 4798 must be re-imported to apply the new prompt and to ingest the post text without the destructive strip.
- **LLM / event_parse defender + escalation POC (bare `<event_type> — <venue>` title)**: opened `INC-2026-05-11-event-parse-defender-and-escalation-poc` to land the first deterministic defender on `parse_event_via_llm`: after Gemma 4 returns, `_event_parse_defender_check` walks the events list and flags any title that matches the bare `<event_type> — <venue>` shape (`Концерт — Бар Бастион`, `Спектакль — Музыкальный театр`, `Лекция — Музей янтаря`) that is already explicitly forbidden by `docs/llm/prompts.md:58` but that Gemma 4 occasionally falls into stochastically (see Bar Bastion and Vasnetsov incidents). When the defender flags anything, the entire batch is re-parsed on `gemini-3.1-flash-lite` (configurable via `EVENT_PARSE_DEFENDER_ESCALATION_MODEL`, empty disables the step) and the new result replaces the original; on escalation timeout the un-escalated Gemma output is returned with a warning log. Quoted-programme titles (`Концерт «Скитальцы»`, `Спектакль «Жили они долго и счастливо»`) are explicitly not flagged. Four new tests in `tests/test_prompt_json.py` pin the defender rule, the per-event reasons list, the escalation flow with `gemma_model=gemini-3.1-flash-lite`, and the no-escalation-on-clean-output case. Live smoke on the Скитальцы source confirms exactly one Gemma call on a clean output (no escalation, ~55s). Future defenders for other principal failures (address-shaped `location_name`, multi-event title clones, etc.) will extend the same `_event_parse_defender_check` helper.
- **Incident / pre-create dup probe missed identical-ticket merge**: opened `INC-2026-05-11-pre-create-dup-probe-missed-identical-ticket-merge` after operator review of production events 4156 and 4752 (both «Жили они долго и счастливо», 2026-05-11 20:00, Дом молодёжи) showed the two rows carrying the **identical** ticket URL `https://actop.us/performances/11-05-zhili-oni-dolgo-i-schastlivo`. Branch 1 of `_pre_create_duplicate_probe` (`identical normalised ticket_link + overlapping date + no time conflict + related titles`) should have merged the second candidate into the existing event but did not. Three plausible root causes — shortlist did not include event 4156 at probe time, candidate `ticket_link` was empty at probe entry, or `_titles_look_related` rejected the obviously related title pair — cannot be distinguished from the post-state evidence alone. Status: **open**. Code-side mitigation deferred; follow-ups include adding structured logging to the probe path (shortlist size, ticket-link match count, per-branch reject reason) and writing a focused replay test under `tests/replays/INC-2026-05-11-pre-create-dup-probe-missed-identical-ticket-merge/` with the two `vk.com/wall-69311452_…` sources. Operator-side action: archive one of the two events through the bot.
- **Locations / Дом молодёжи aliases (event 4582 / 4156 / 4752 dup family)**: extended `docs/reference/location-aliases.md` with twelve aliases that normalise common ways of referring to the «Дом молодёжи» building at Октябрьская 76 in Калининград — including the address-only forms (`Ул. Октябрьская, 76`, `Октябрьская 76`, `октябрьская, 76`, …) and the in-house theatre brand (`Театр современной драмы Акт.Опус`, `Актопус`, `Акт.Опус`, …). Motivation: production event 4582 was created with `location_name="Ул. Октябрьская, 76"` (a literal address in the venue-name field, a master-prompt rule violation) because there was no alias to snap any address-only mention of `Октябрьская 76` to the canonical Дом молодёжи row, and event 4156 was kept on the theatre brand instead of the building. Operator-side action: archive the legacy duplicates 4156 and 4582 through the bot; future imports for this venue should normalise to `Дом молодёжи, Октябрьская 76, Калининград` on either name path.
- **Incident / standup-excursion meeting point snapped to nearby Known venue**: opened `INC-2026-05-11-standup-excursion-meeting-point-snapped-to-zoo` after operator review of production event 4687 («Стендап-Экскурсия по Калининграду», 2026-05-11 14:00) found `location_name="Калининградский зоопарк"` + `location_address="просп. Мира 2"` while the source post explicitly says the meeting point is `Скульптура "Борющиеся зубры" просп. Мира, 2 / остановка "Технический университет"`. The bull sculpture and the zoo sit on the same avenue but at different addresses (the zoo is at пр-т Мира 26, not 2), and the zoo is not mentioned anywhere in the post. A 2026-05-11 probe of the unchanged master prompt reproduced the bug deterministically — Gemma 4 was snapping the meeting-point address to the geographically closest "Known venues" entry, ignoring the address mismatch. Fix: added a "Meeting-point override" bullet to `docs/llm/prompts.md` near the venue-grounding rules (line 113 area), explicitly listing the meeting-point markers (`Встреча:`/`Место встречи:`/`Сбор:`/`Точка старта:`/`Встречаемся у/возле/около/на`) and the landmark family (sculpture/памятник, остановка, площадь, ворота, мост, фонтан, угол улиц, park entrance) for which the override applies, with the 4687 example as a forbidden anti-pattern. Re-probe after the fix returned `location_name="Скульптура «Борющиеся зубры»"`, `location_address=""`, `city="Калининград"` — accepted per operator framing (the rule allows either the landmark form or the empty form with city only). Regression test `tests/test_prompt_json.py::test_base_prompt_includes_meeting_point_override_rule` pins the rule text. Event 4687 still needs a manual re-import via Smart Update to apply the new prompt output to the public card.
- **Incident / poster near-duplicate kept + semantically relevant tram photo dropped**: opened `INC-2026-05-11-poster-near-duplicate-and-tram-photo-dropped` after operator review of production event 4727 («Аудиоспектакль "Путешествие налегке"», Telegraph `Audiospektakl-Puteshestvie-nalegke-05-08`) showed the published page rendering cover + two near-duplicate copies of the VK poster (Supabase URLs `d067…0022a.webp` and `d067…0002a.webp`, byte-different but pixel-near-identical with OCR misread `налегке` vs `на плечах` on the same image) while the tram photo `C75B5B35-…-big.jpg` (OCR `в ДЕПО`) — the natural visual anchor for an «аудиоспектакль в театральном трамвае» — was silently dropped by per-poster scoring. Root cause is two layered defects: (a) `_select_eventposter_render_urls` ([main.py:1907-1958](main.py)) groups posters by OCR signature; the phash fallback at line 1953 only activates when OCR is absent, and `EventPoster.phash` is populated for only 1 of 6 rows on this event anyway, so OCR-divergent near-duplicates from one VK upload survive into the render set; (b) `_score_eventposter_against_event` ([main.py:2028-2111](main.py)) is token-only, so the tram photo gets score 0 against the event title and is filtered out by the `score >= max(2.0, best_score-2.0)` cut at `main.py:16502`. Status: open — fix deferred to backlog as a two-layer pipeline change (mandatory phash + LLM `poster_relevance` judge for near-threshold low-score posters). No code change in this CHANGELOG bullet; the incident record carries the regression contract.
- **Incident / lecturer name+title silently dropped from description**: opened `INC-2026-05-11-lecturer-name-and-title-dropped-from-description` after operator review of production event 4759 (`Лекция главного архитектора Калининграда`, Telegram source `t.me/kraftmarket39/219`) found the public Telegraph description missing both the lecturer's name (`Андрей Анисимов`) and the explicit job title (`главный архитектор Калининграда`), even though the source post contained both in a dedicated `О спикере` section. Root cause: the `rich_facts_extract` `people_org_facts` rule was generic and let Gemma 4 collapse `<Имя>, <должность>` into the impersonal paraphrase `Профессиональная позиция спикера находится на пересечении градостроительной политики…`, stripping the load-bearing identity. Fix: tightened the `people_org_facts` bullet in the `rich_facts_extract` prompt ([smart_event_update.py:6973+](smart_event_update.py)) to require keeping ИМЯ and ДОЛЖНОСТЬ/РЕГАЛИИ in one named fact (`Лектор: Андрей Анисимов, главный архитектор Калининграда`), explicitly forbid the `профессиональная позиция спикера…` / `спикер представит позицию…` collapse, and treat dedicated `О спикере`/`Лектор:`/`Спикер:`/`Ведущий:`/`Автор:` sections as load-bearing. Two regression tests in `tests/test_smart_update_native_schema.py` pin (a) the rule text against the real 4759 source excerpt and (b) that a properly named `people_org_facts` entry survives `_flatten_g4_rich_facts_payload` into `facts_text_clean`. Event 4759 still needs a manual re-import via Smart Update so the new prompt regenerates its `event_source_fact` ledger and public description.
- **Incident / Vasnetsov 30 May stochastic title clone**: opened `INC-2026-05-11-vasnetsov-30may-stochastic-title-clone` after operator review found production event 4761 (`2026-05-30 16:00` at `Сигнал`) carrying the title `Виктор Васнецов: богатырь, написавший русскую сказку` while the source Telegram post `t.me/signalkld/10657` is a multi-event digest by «Культур-Мультур» announcing **two** lectures (15 May 19:00 Васнецов + 30 May 16:00 `Зинаида Серебрякова: нежность, пережившая катастрофу`). `event_parse` correctly split the dates but cloned the first event's title onto the second event, and `rich_facts_extract` even noted the parallel Серебрякова lecture in a fact while still leaving the title wrong. A 2026-05-11 probe of `parse_event_via_llm` on the unchanged master prompt returned the correct two distinct titles, confirming the failure is a stochastic Gemma 4 lapse on this single import, not a deterministic prompt gap. No code change landed; closure relies on operator-side re-import of event 4761 via the bot's admin delete path so the source post is parsed again. A secondary anomaly is flagged for follow-up: the same source produced two `event_source` rows (1826481, 1826482) 90 seconds apart.
- **Incident / Bar Bastion stochastic `Концерт — <venue>` title and semantic duplicate**: opened `INC-2026-05-11-bar-bastion-stochastic-title-fallback-and-semantic-dup` after operator review flagged production events 4765 (`2026-08-15 20:00`) and 4788 (`2026-07-04 19:00`), both at `Бар Бастион`, with the explicitly forbidden bare title `Концерт — Бар Бастион` (`docs/llm/prompts.md:58`). Both source posts had clearly title-eligible programme info (`тур «Скитальцы»`/`Артур БЕРКУТ` + `Сергей МАВРИН` and `«БАСТИОН», опен-эйр ДЕНЬ ГОРОДА`), and a fresh probe of `parse_event_via_llm` on event 4765's source returned the correct `🎸 Концерт «Скитальцы»: Артур Беркут и Сергей Маврин`, confirming a stochastic Gemma 4 fallback rather than a prompt gap. Event 4765 is additionally a semantic duplicate of legitimate event 4486 (`Скитальцы: Сергей Маврин и Артур Беркут`, 2026-08-15 21:00 at `Бар Бастион`); `_pre_create_duplicate_probe` did not merge because `ticket_link`s differ (`qtickets.events/232664-…` vs `vk.cc/cXi4Uk`) and times differ (20:00 vs 21:00). No code change landed; closure relies on operator-side delete of event 4765 (dup) and event 4788 (bad title) via the bot. A semantic dup-probe extension (same date + same venue + similar time ±2h + related programme cue) is captured as an open follow-up.
- **LLM / Smart Update G4 `rich_facts_extract` named-speaker rule**: tightened the `people_org_facts` rule in the `rich_facts_extract` prompt ([smart_event_update.py:6973+](smart_event_update.py)) so that named lecturers/speakers/hosts/guests/authors with an explicit job title or credentials (главный архитектор, профессор, режиссёр-постановщик, кандидат наук, художественный руководитель, член Союза …) must be preserved as one named fact like `Лектор: Андрей Анисимов, главный архитектор Калининграда`. Model is explicitly forbidden from collapsing `<Имя>, <должность>` into impersonal paraphrases such as `профессиональная позиция спикера…` or `спикер представит позицию…`, and a dedicated `О спикере`/`Лектор:`/`Спикер:`/`Ведущий:`/`Автор:` section must yield a named fact whenever it contains either a name or a title. Motivation: prod event 4759 (`Лекция главного архитектора Калининграда`, source `t.me/kraftmarket39/219`) had both the speaker name (`Андрей Анисимов`) and the title in the source post, but `rich_facts_extract` produced an impersonal `Профессиональная позиция спикера находится на пересечении…` fact that stripped both, leaving the public Telegraph description with no mention of who reads the lecture. Two regression tests in `tests/test_smart_update_native_schema.py` pin (a) the new rule text in the prompt against the real 4759 source excerpt and (b) that a properly named `people_org_facts` entry survives `_flatten_g4_rich_facts_payload` into `facts_text_clean`. See `INC-2026-05-11-lecturer-name-and-title-dropped-from-description` for the full incident record. Event 4759 itself still needs a manual re-import via Smart Update to regenerate `event_source_fact` and `description` against the corrected prompt.
- **CherryFlash / story fanout adds `@catwithbag`**: extended the ordered cherryflash story fanout in `video_announce/scenario.py::_popular_review_selection_params.story_targets_override` to repost to `@catwithbag` after `@loving_guide39` with the standard `600` second delay and `mode=repost_previous`, matching the existing channel-chain pattern. New chain: `@kenigevents → @lovekenig (+600s) → @loving_guide39 (+600s) → @catwithbag (+600s) → encrypted Telegram Business targets`. Canonical doc `docs/features/cherryflash/README.md` updated (current-state bullet + acceptance checklist line). Userbot is already an admin in `@catwithbag` per operator confirmation, so no runtime preflight change was needed.
- **LLM / `event_parse` large-post pre-routing + provider-level timeout**: `parse_event_via_llm` (in `main.py`, the entry every VK / Telegram / guide-excursions auto-import goes through to split a post into event drafts) now pre-routes oversized posts directly to a different model instead of waiting for Gemma 4's thought-loop / 5xx storm before fallback. New env: `EVENT_PARSE_LARGE_POST_THRESHOLD_CHARS` (default `2500`) and `EVENT_PARSE_LARGE_POST_MODEL` (default `gemini-3.1-flash-lite`). Threshold counts `len(text) + len(poster_summary) + sum(len(poster_texts))`; size, not provider-reported `input_tokens`, is used because the system prompt (~6000 tokens of venues/holidays/rules) dominates `input_tokens` and would mask the per-post signal. Motivation: a 2026-05-09 A/B on real prod fixtures showed Gemma 4 entering thought-loop on a 2587-char 9 May digest (~32k thought tokens, no output, hangs for 15 minutes — the same failure mode that killed `wall-69311452_2727` against the live `EVENT_PARSE_GEMMA_WALL_CLOCK_SEC=240` cap), while `gemini-3.1-flash-lite` parsed the same digest into 15 grounded drafts in 11s. Posts below the threshold (typical single-event VK posts) keep Gemma 4 as primary because it's faster on small payloads and we want to spread the daily Gemini Flash Lite RPD budget. Routing is logged via `event_parse: large_post route ... primary=...` so operators can see in `/data/runtime_logs/events-bot.log` which posts went where. Set threshold to `0` or `EVENT_PARSE_LARGE_POST_MODEL=` to disable. Paired with `GOOGLE_AI_PROVIDER_TIMEOUT_SEC=120` (per-call timeout that turns hangs into retryable TimeoutError so `_build_model_chain` can transparently swap models) and `GOOGLE_AI_FALLBACK_MODELS=gemini-3.1-flash-lite,gemma-4-31b-it` (bidirectional fallback chain — Smart Update Gemini-primary stages fall back to Gemma 4 on Gemini outages, while Gemma-primary stages including `event_parse` fall back to Gemini on Gemma 5xx/timeouts).
- **LLM / Smart Update facts+writer primary moved to `gemini-3.1-flash-lite`**: the two heaviest user-visible Smart Update stages — `facts_extract` / `rich_facts_extract` and the main writer (`fact_first_desc`, `split_description_writer`) — now route to `gemini-3.1-flash-lite` first and fall back to `gemma-4-31b-it` through the existing `GoogleAIClient` model chain (set `GOOGLE_AI_FALLBACK_MODELS=gemma-4-31b-it` on prod). Motivation: a 2026-05-09 production probe across all three Google API keys (`GOOGLE_API_KEY`, `GOOGLE_API_KEY2`, `GOOGLE_API_KEY3`) showed `gemma-4-31b-it` mass-returning instant `500 INTERNAL` (~58% of single requests, all keys, all configs — schema/no-schema/json-mime), while `gemini-3.1-flash-lite` succeeded on 6/6 probes and matched Gemma's writer/facts quality on real VK fixtures (slightly more verbose prose; same factual grounding). Other Smart Update LLM stages (coverage, revise, rewrite, short_description, search_digest, lollipop, reflow, poster_relevance) are intentionally **not** moved — they keep `SMART_UPDATE_MODEL` so this rollout stays a minimal-blast-radius switch on exactly the two stages the user asked for. Routing is controlled by new env overrides `SMART_UPDATE_FACTS_MODEL` / `SMART_UPDATE_WRITER_MODEL` (defaults: `gemini-3.1-flash-lite`); set them to `gemma-4-31b-it` to roll back without redeploy. New limiter row: `migrations/005_gemini_flash_lite_limits.sql` registers `gemini-3.1-flash-lite` in `google_ai_model_limits` with a defensive 10% safety margin under the AI Studio published Free Tier cap (`RPM=13/15`, `RPD=450/500`, `TPM=240000/250000`) so the Supabase reserve never approaches the hard daily cap that would lock the key for ~24h. Headroom on three keys at current load (~30 events/day, peak 67) is `≥3×` even when each event triggers 2 update passes; fallback to Gemma exists for Google-side outages on the lite model. Per-stage model is logged in `smart_update: gemma {json,text}_call ... model=...` and in the `GoogleAIClient` JSON line (`requested_model` / `provider_model_name` / `invoked_model`) so post-rollout observation of routing and latency is straightforward.
- **Ops / `parse_debug` log cleanup**: removed 127 stale per-run `source_parsing_*.log` files (>30 days old) from `/data/parse_debug` on prod; this directory has a writer (`source_parsing/handlers.py`) but no readers in the codebase and no rotation, so it had been silently growing since January. Freed ~10 MB; `/data` went from `47%` to `46%`. `runtime_logs` already has a working `TimedRotatingFileHandler` with 24h retention and was not touched.
- **Incident / May 9 event venue alias, free-status, phone and duplicate regressions**: opened `INC-2026-05-09-event-location-alias-free-dup-regressions` after operator review found a new batch of public Telegraph/daily quality defects: wrong or incomplete venues/addresses (`#МЫВМЕСТЕ`, `Любовь по-итальянски`, `Шаманское путешествие`, `Социальный рупор`, `Военный парад`), venue alias drift (`Сигнал`, `Кинозал`, `Научная библиотека`, missing bar aliases), poster/OCR address loss, uncertain or false free labels, duplicate cards (`Музей изнутри`, `Будь здоров, школяр!`, `Нам нужна одна Победа`), prose in `location_name`, and phone-only contact rendering. The first prevention pass keeps the path LLM-first: the existing separate Telegram Monitoring Gemma 4 `location_review` native-schema stage now also triggers when extracted venue fields are ungrounded but source/OCR/context has venue or address cues, while `docs/reference/locations.md` / `docs/reference/location-aliases.md` gained verified reference rows/aliases for `Дворец спорта «Янтарный»`, `Шоурум Mysig`, `Бар Дредноут`, `Сигнал`, `ИЦАЭ`, and `Научная библиотека` variants. `location_reference` now force-applies curated aliases across `location_name/location_address/city`, matching the existing main parser wrapper. Replay fixture: `tests/replays/INC-2026-05-09-event-location-alias-free-dup-regressions/sources.json`; targeted reference checks: `4 passed`.
- **CherryFlash / ИЦАЭ Partner Track (spec)**: documented planned `partner_icae_001` / `icae_events` CherryFlash Business Story track in `docs/features/cherryflash/partner-story-tracks.md`. Repo-visible docs intentionally avoid the raw Telegram handle and refer to the target only through the encrypted Business target contract.
- **Incident / VK Quality False Skips**: opened `INC-2026-05-08-vk-quality-false-skips` after production VK auto-import / Smart Update lost three real VK events and published one Telegraph proper-name typo: event 4717 used `Симюран` instead of source-grounded `Симуран`; `wall-48383763_40430` was skipped as `non_event_notice` despite a paid zoo excursion; `wall-78248807_6006` was rejected as `unknown_region` despite Fort 11 Doenhoff being in Kaliningrad; `wall-138053522_2532` was skipped as `online_event` despite describing an offline art market. The fix keeps the path LLM-first: VK Gemma 4 draft prompting now treats one-place/one-day art-market or holiday programmes as one umbrella event unless independent anchors prove separate events; Smart Update narrows `non_event_notice` and `online_event` only for LLM-grounded physical candidates; Fort 11 Doenhoff was added to canonical locations/aliases; and source-grounded known-spelling restoration protects the `Симуран` surname. Regression fixture: `tests/replays/INC-2026-05-08-vk-quality-false-skips/sources.json`; targeted checks: `19 passed` across Smart Update guard/location tests.
- **Incident / VK + TG May 8 prompt regressions and missing dup probe**: opened `INC-2026-05-08-vk-tg-prompt-and-dup-probe` after the May 8 `/daily` surface shipped with eight quality regressions visible in production: (a) duplicate cards for `Окна Победы` (4173/4200), `Ансамбль Балтийского флота` (2819/4038), `Дегустация сыров и вин` (4350/4396/4397), `Барн "Малыш"` (4584/4585), `Эдит Пиаф` (3824/3983); (b) `location_address` hallucinations — `Потемкина 20 asignatura` on event 4397, `ТРЦ "Европа" 2 этаж` as venue address for `Янтарь холл` on events 4038/3983 (`ТРЦ "Европа"` is the box-office point, not the venue), 200-char curator-quote prose in `location_name` on 4584/4585; (c) bare `Концерт — Янтарь холл` title on event 4670 because the source post had no explicit programme name; (d) hallucinated content on event 4645 («Мастер-класс по созданию ёлочных игрушек» description fused unrelated DJ/90s programme fragments); (e) two May 15 events `4664`/`4705` published into `/daily` without `telegraph_url`. Auto-import run on 2026-05-08 06:14–08:05 also surfaced repeated Gemma 4 `500 INTERNAL` provider errors on `event_parse`, a `TimeoutError ... 0.0s` on `telegraph_render_remove_logistics`, and one row pinned for the full 1800s `VK_AUTO_IMPORT_ROW_TIMEOUT_SEC`. LLM-first hotfix landed on this branch: master prompt now forbids foreign-language tokens, ticket-sales points, and prose in `location_address` (with explicit `Янтарь холл` ↔ `ТРЦ "Европа"` separation rule), forbids the `<event_type> — <venue>` title fallback (return `[]` when neither name nor programme theme is recoverable), and `smart_event_update.py` gained a deterministic `_pre_create_duplicate_probe` last-line safety net before `INSERT event` — gated to non-`parser:*` sources, with two branches (identical normalised `ticket_link` + overlapping `date` + no time conflict + `_titles_look_related`; identical normalised `location_name` + overlapping `date` + identical non-empty time anchor + `_titles_look_related`). Probe is covered by 7 tests in `tests/test_pre_create_duplicate_probe.py`. Follow-ups still pending: (1) wrap `main._parse_event_via_gemma` in `asyncio.wait_for(EVENT_PARSE_GEMMA_WALL_CLOCK_SEC)` so a Gemma 4 5xx storm cannot consume the row timeout; (2) locate the description-truncation step that cut event 3983 to `> Спектакль-байопик «Эдит Пиаф.`; (3) investigate `joboutbox::telegraph_build` failures for events 4664/4705; (4) compensating data remediation (archive duplicates, reset `vk_inbox` for 4670/4645).
- **Telegraph / VK / Telegram: phone-only ticket as visible contact line**: when `event.ticket_link` is a `tel:+...` URI (the case for events whose only point of registration is a phone number; produced by the Gemma 4 vk_intake `phone-as-tel` rule from `INC-2026-05-07-vk-auto-import-merge-regression-gemma4`), the public infoblock and the `/daily` Telegram / VK renderers now emit the phone as a separate `📞 +7 (XXX) XXX-XX-XX` line instead of trying to wrap the word `Билеты` in a `<a href="tel:...">` anchor. Telegraph silently strips `tel:` from `<a href>` (only `http`/`https`/`mailto` survive its allowlist), so the previous rendering collapsed to a plain `Билеты` word with no clickable phone — confirmed on the live page for event 4684 (`https://telegra.ph/Semejnyj-den-na-prirode-v-AgroParke-Nekrasovo-pole-05-07-2`). New shared helper `markup.format_tel_link_for_display` formats `tel:` URIs as Russian-style `+7 (911) 474-30-04` strings; covered by `tests/test_format_tel_link_for_display.py`. The historical `feat(telegraph): add clickable phone numbers (tel: links)` (commit `4b8b2114`) auto-linkifier in `linkify_for_telegraph` is now redundant for Telegraph (Telegraph drops the produced anchor anyway) but is left in place because it still helps legacy paths that don't go through Telegraph.
- **CherryFlash / Partner Story Tracks (spec)**: landed `docs/features/cherryflash/partner-story-tracks.md` as the canonical product specification for partner-specific CherryFlash story releases over Telegram Business Stories. Status is explicitly `Product specification / not implemented in code yet`. Captures the per-partner track model (content/geo filters, source allow/deny, render profile, schedule overrides), the privacy / encrypted-target contract (`connection_hash`/`user_hash`/`username_hash`/`partner_id`, no raw handles or `business_connection_id` in repo/code/env/Kaggle), and the rollout plan that builds on the existing `telegram_business.load_business_story_targets` / `setting.video_announce_story_business_targets` infrastructure. The CherryFlash README now cross-links to this spec.
- **Incident / VK Auto-Import Merge Regression on Gemma 4**: opened `INC-2026-05-07-vk-auto-import-merge-regression-gemma4` after VK auto-import created `event` 4679 (`Портрет девушки-бойца`, 2026-05-08 19:00 at `Научная библиотека`) as a duplicate of the existing 4445 (`Концерт «Портрет девушки-бойца»`) sharing the **identical** ticket_link, while same-day VK posts also showed thinned writer descriptions, a leaked `&quot;` HTML entity in `event.description.4679`, a hallucinated `Киноленд, Киевская 71` venue on event 4681 (source: «бывшая пивоварня Понарт»), a lost-only-contact phone (`8-967-356-9479`) for event 4681, and a production-blocker on `wall-194968_17449` (4-spectacle digest post) where Gemma 4 vk_intake collapsed four bullets into one fabricated `🎭 ПИРОСМАНИ` candidate with `city=Пятигорск` + Калининград venue, the merge LLM hallucinated a Bach/Mozart/Beethoven program (rejected by grounding guards), then the Gemma merge call timed out on `500 INTERNAL` for ~15 minutes (`duration_ms=920763`) and the 4o fallback never wrote `vk_inbox.status` back, leaving the row stuck at `pending`. Production-data hand-fixed: 4679 archived (`lifecycle_status=cancelled`, VK source moved to 4445); `vk_inbox.id=6728` flipped to `status='rejected'`; backups in `incident_vk_merge_regression_backup_20260507`. `ENABLE_RUNTIME_FILE_LOGGING=1` enabled on prod for evidence collection. Reference docs `docs/reference/locations.md` and `docs/reference/kaliningrad_oblast_places.md` updated to seed `АгроПарк «Некрасово поле»` and `Некрасово` for event 4678 address recovery. Code-side hardening landed on this branch: `SMART_UPDATE_GEMMA_JSON_WALL_CLOCK_SEC` (default `90s`) wraps every `_ask_gemma_json` call so a provider 500-storm cannot pin smart_update; matching `SMART_UPDATE_GEMMA_TEXT_WALL_CLOCK_SEC` (default `120s`) bounds `_ask_gemma_text`; `vk_intake.parse_event_via_llm` now `html.unescape`s VK post text and OCR poster text before prompt input so source HTML entities cannot leak into writer output; `smart_event_update.smart_update_event` now runs a deterministic post-decision duplicate probe on `(date,time,location_name,ticket_link)` parity before `INSERT`, switching to merge with `match_reason='post_decision_ticket_anchor'`; vk_intake parser schema gained `is_multi_event_digest` so digest-only posts are skipped at vk_inbox without sending a single LLM-fabricated candidate to smart_update; vk_intake prompt rewrote location/title rules under Gemma 4 contract — anti-fabrication for `location_name`/`location_address`, format-anchor preservation in title (`мастер-класс`/`лекция`/`спектакль`/`концерт`), phone-only contact captured as `ticket_link='tel:<digits>'`. Existing remediation evidence is in `artifacts/codex/INC-2026-05-07-vk-merge-regression-gemma4/`.
- **Incident / VK Time Reschedule Wrong Match**: opened `INC-2026-05-07-vk-time-reschedule-wrong-match` after `/vk_auto_import 1` treated `8 мая время начала Винного вечера перенесено на 19.30` as a cancellation/postponement and marked unrelated old `event_id=2029` inactive. `vk_auto_queue` now keeps explicit start-time reschedule notices on the normal LLM-first VK import path, parses Russian month-name dates locally without importing Smart Update, continues past invalid `dd.mm` candidates such as `19.30`, and refuses cancellation matching when both date and title anchors are absent.
- **LLM / Smart Update G4 Lollipop-Light Writer v3 + Venue Leak Suppression**: tightened the Gemma 4 lollipop-light final writer system prompt to `final_writer.v3` so it (1) emits `- item` bullets only for sections whose writer_pack actually has non-empty `literal_items`, and (2) merges several narrative facts that differ only by a named entity (composer/performer/work/role) into one compact sentence with comma-separated names instead of repeating the surrounding phrase. This prevents the previous "одно-слово / повторяющийся префикс" bullet spam (`- произведения Баха / - произведения Шуберта / …`) while keeping legitimate program/repertoire literal lists unchanged. Additionally, `_g4_lollipop_light_normalize_bucket_payload` now suppresses short standalone venue brand names (e.g. `Сигнал`, `Дом`) from the writer-pack `infoblock` constraint when `location_name` has no address-word/digit/multi-comma signals, so the writer-pack leak validator no longer raises false-positive `infoblock.leak:LG03` and triggers a useless gemma4 retry — the existing narrative-strip path already protects natural mentions like «в Сигнале». Address-like `location_name` values (street word + number + commas) keep the leak guard intact. Captured by new tests `test_g4_lollipop_light_normalize_bucket_suppresses_short_venue_name_from_infoblock` and `test_g4_lollipop_light_normalize_bucket_keeps_address_like_location_in_infoblock`. Full-pipeline VK upstream smoke on the harder PRODDB-4594 fixture (`artifacts/codex/smart_update_g4_upstream_vk_smoke_4594_*`) confirms `raw source_text -> build_event_drafts_from_vk -> EventCandidate -> smart_event_update lollipop-light` on Gemma 4, status `created`, sane anchors, no `4o` token spent (`FOUR_O_TOKEN` / `FOUR_4O_TOKEN` / `OPENAI_*` scrubbed before run).
- **LLM / Smart Update Gemma 3 Flow Audit**: moved the shared VK/TG draft parser default from `gemma-3-27b-it` to `gemma-4-31b-it`, disabled its implicit JSON-error fallback to 4o unless `EVENT_PARSE_ENABLE_4O_FALLBACK=1`, and moved Smart Update's region-filter LLM fallback to `gemma-4-31b-it` via `GEO_REGION_GEMMA_MODEL`. This closes production-relevant Gemma 3 defaults ahead of the lollipop-light Smart Update benchmark without adding regex-based semantic extraction. The follow-up Gemma-only lollipop-light replay (`smart_update_g4_full_surface_benchmark_20260507T111913Z`) created all three known fixtures with `0` field/infoblock diffs and no 4o calls; manual review keeps it gated on dense-list writer quality (`4594`) and logistics duplication (`4538`). A narrow lollipop-light prompt/cleanup pass now discourages one-word bullets for interests/examples and strips hard logistics after the final writer; the real VK upstream smoke (`smart_update_g4_upstream_vk_smoke_20260507T115350Z.recovered`) confirmed `source_text -> build_event_drafts_from_vk -> smart_event_update` on Gemma 4 for `4598`, with correct persisted anchors and no 4o call.
- **LLM / Smart Update G4 Full-Surface Lollipop-Light Candidate**: added an off-by-default production create-path switch `SMART_UPDATE_G4_LOLLIPOP_LIGHT_CREATE=1` that keeps the heavy `create_bundle` fact extraction, then runs lollipop-light bucket/prioritize/layout stages through Gemma 4 native schema and writes the final public body through `SMART_UPDATE_G4_LOLLIPOP_LIGHT_WRITER_LANE=gemma4|4o|adaptive`. Dense adaptive packs can route to one explicit final `4o` call (`SMART_UPDATE_G4_LOLLIPOP_LIGHT_4O_FACT_THRESHOLD`, default `14`), while smaller packs stay on Gemma 4; if the 4o writer output fails validation, the path may repair with a Gemma-only correction pass without spending a second 4o request. The light native stages now retry transient provider errors once (`SMART_UPDATE_G4_LOLLIPOP_LIGHT_STAGE_RETRIES`, default `2`) and the benchmark can replay older prod snapshots with `--allow-past-events`. Full-surface sandbox evidence on the current prod snapshot keeps persisted fields/infoblocks at `0 diff`: `4598` accepted `16/16`, `4538` accepted `11/11`, and the tightened dense `4594` run improved to `21/22` with only one minor loss; the first local adaptive `4o` full-surface proof used exactly one `writer.final_4o` call but failed text validation, so adaptive `4o` remains gated behind writer prompt/repair evidence.
- **LLM / Smart Update G4 Adaptive Lollipop-Light Benchmark**: `scripts/inspect/benchmark_smart_update_g4_stages.py` now supports `--writer-lane gemma4|4o|adaptive` with an adaptive dense-fact threshold and explicit `writer_lane_selected` / `four_o_calls_observed` reporting. The production create-bundle fact contract was tightened to preserve organizers, goals, methodology/background, and exact numbers before decorative facts, and the non-split create-bundle fact cap increased from `18` to `24`. Latest prod-snapshot staged evidence (`smart_update_g4_stage_benchmark_20260507T030037Z`) keeps `PRODDB-4594` on one final `gpt-4o` writer call and leaves the smaller `4598` / `4538` cases on Gemma 4: `4594` improves to `21/22` grounded facts with `0` major losses, `4598` stays `14/16` with no critical/major/minor losses, and `4538` remains accepted at `11/11`.
- **LLM / Smart Update G4 Split Create v2**: expanded the off-by-default `SMART_UPDATE_G4_SPLIT_CREATE=1` probe into `g4-split-create-v2-rich-facts`: the critical extraction stage now uses a richer sectioned native-schema payload (`rich_facts_extract`) with a larger source budget and up to 40 persisted candidate facts; the main body is written by the separate, responsible `split_description_writer`; and lightweight `split_derived_fields` owns only `short_description` / `search_digest`. `rich_facts_extract` remains the heavy fact-ledger stage and may fall back from native schema to prompt schema when the provider rejects native output; `split_description_writer` is capped by `SMART_UPDATE_G4_DESCRIPTION_WRITER_TIMEOUT_SEC` (default `90`), while `split_derived_fields` is capped by `SMART_UPDATE_G4_DERIVED_FIELDS_TIMEOUT_SEC` (default `20`). The description writer does not double-call via prompt-schema fallback unless `SMART_UPDATE_G4_SPLIT_CREATE_PROMPT_FALLBACK=1` is explicitly set, while the lightweight derived-fields stage may use prompt-schema fallback. Split-create stages disable the legacy 4o fallback so Gemma 4 migration benchmarks stay model-clean. If the description writer is unavailable after facts are extracted, create uses a bounded fact-ledger fallback instead of the legacy multi-step fact-first/rewrite/reflow cascade. The split cleanup now strips hard infoblock duplicates without rejecting route words such as streets/districts in excursion copy. The full-surface benchmark gained `--skip-llm-reviewer` so inner-loop probes do not spend extra Gemma calls on fact review; artifacts remain suitable for direct manual/Codex comparison.
- **LLM / Smart Update G4 Split Create Probe**: added off-by-default `SMART_UPDATE_G4_SPLIT_CREATE=1` experiment (`g4-split-create-v1`). It disables the heavy combined `match_create_bundle` / `create_bundle` create path, extracts `facts_text_clean` first, then asks a smaller `split_create_writer` JSON stage for `description`, `short_description`, and `search_digest`; if the writer fails, the existing fact-first fallback still owns description generation.
- **LLM / Smart Update G4 Latency Diagnostics**: added per-call Smart Update LLM tracing to the full-surface benchmark so artifacts show stage labels, durations, statuses, retry/provider-error counts, and top latency contributors. Added an opt-in `SMART_UPDATE_FACT_FIRST_TIMEOUT_SEC` guard for diagnostic fail-open runs of fact-first description generation; it defaults to `0` because the first 90s timeout probe sped up two fixtures but regressed fact coverage on one (`16/16 -> 15/16`) and the third fixture still exposed a separate long `create_bundle` provider call after repeated `500` errors.
- **LLM / Smart Update Gemma Retry Bound**: Smart Update Gemma wrappers now default `SMART_UPDATE_GEMMA_RETRIES` to `1` because `GoogleAIClient` already retries retryable provider failures. This prevents one failing `create_bundle` / fact-first stage from expanding into up to `9` provider calls; operators can still raise `SMART_UPDATE_GEMMA_RETRIES` explicitly for local probes.
- **LLM / Smart Update G4 Native Schema Latency Probe**: `event_topics` now uses `gemma-4-31b-it` native `response_schema` by default with prompt-schema fallback, cutting the three-fixture topics probe from `71.824s` to `11.099s` while preserving `3/3` success. Added off-by-default Smart Update JSON-stage native schema support (`SMART_UPDATE_GEMMA_NATIVE_SCHEMA=1`, default stages `facts_extract,create_bundle`) for follow-up full-surface benchmarks; it remains gated because `smart_update_g4_full_surface_benchmark_20260506T141044Z` was faster on two fixtures but regressed fact coverage (`15/22 -> 12/22`, `16/16 -> 13/16`) and became slower/reviewer-blocked on the third fixture.
- **LLM / Smart Update G4 Full-Surface Sandbox Benchmark**: added `scripts/inspect/benchmark_smart_update_g4_full_surfaces.py`, which copies a production SQLite snapshot into `artifacts/codex/`, recreates selected events through the real `smart_event_update()` create path on `gemma-4-31b-it`, and compares persisted event fields / infoblock, `event_source_fact`, `facts_text_clean`, Telegraph body, `short_description`, and `search_digest`. Current three-fixture evidence (`smart_update_g4_full_surface_benchmark_20260506T094705Z`) has `0` field/infoblock diffs, but only `1/3` accepted fact coverage and unacceptable latency/provider stability.
- **LLM / Smart Update And Event Topics Gemma 4 Defaults**: `SMART_UPDATE_MODEL` and `EVENT_TOPICS_MODEL` now default to `gemma-4-31b-it` instead of the unavailable Gemma 3 model. Event topics no longer inherit `TG_MONITORING_TEXT_MODEL`, keeping the Smart Update persistence benchmark on the same Gemma 4 runtime path instead of disabling topic classification.
- **LLM / Smart Update G4 Benchmark Coverage Surfaces**: `scripts/inspect/benchmark_smart_update_g4_stages.py` now reports candidate logistics / infoblock facts separately from `facts_text_clean`, carrying logistics-like raw facts plus canonical date/time/venue/address/city/free/ticket/price/Pushkin-card fields into the fact-coverage payload. Reviewer provider failures are now surfaced as `review_error` instead of being normalized into a false `accepted` verdict.
- **LLM / Smart Update G4 Prod-DB Benchmark**: `scripts/inspect/benchmark_smart_update_g4_stages.py` now runs multi-fixture staged comparisons from a local production SQLite snapshot via `--prod-db --event-ids`, using frozen Gemma 3-era production `description` / `short_description` / `search_digest` / `event_source_fact` as baseline and source evidence only for the Gemma 4 candidate. Reports now fence generated markdown descriptions, include baseline/candidate Telegraph previews with infoblock/search/description, checkpoint after each completed fixture, and run the final writer as primary `Gemma 4` (`writer.final_g4_primary`, `four_o_calls_observed=0`) while keeping the old 4o-primary branch commented for rollback.
- **LLM Gateway / Google GenAI SDK Migration**: `GoogleAIClient` now prefers the current `google.genai` SDK for provider calls and keeps deprecated `google.generativeai` only as a lazy compatibility fallback. The Smart Update G4 benchmark routes Gemma JSON stages through this shared gateway instead of direct legacy SDK calls, and benchmark imports no longer emit the `google.generativeai` deprecation warning.
- **LLM / Smart Update G4 Frozen Baseline Benchmark**: `scripts/inspect/benchmark_smart_update_g4_stages.py` now supports `--reuse-baseline-artifact`, loading preserved Gemma 3 baseline facts/text/timings from `artifacts/codex/lollipop_g4_benchmark_20260501T212029Z.json` or a previous single-fixture staged artifact instead of trying to rerun the now-unavailable Gemma 3 baseline. The generated staged report records `baseline_path=frozen_current_smart_update_baseline` and `baseline_source_artifact=...`; candidate generation remains baseline-free.
- **LLM / Smart Update G4 Single-Fixture Recovery**: tightened the G4 create-bundle prompt so object/craft exhibition facts preserve source-local qualities (technique, secret production details, visual signature, diversity/uniqueness, freedom of execution, typical plots/forms) and derived-field prompts avoid CTA/promo register. The staged benchmark now reuses valid bundle `short_description` / `search_digest` before spending extra Gemma calls, and falls back to a compact structured Gemma 4 writer payload when final `4o` is unavailable. Latest `RED-COSMOS` evidence (`artifacts/codex/smart_update_g4_stage_benchmark_20260502T145722Z.{md,json}`) reaches accepted gates for facts (`12/12` baseline covered, `0` lost), final description (`747` chars, `4` semantic headings, `7` bullets, no writer validation errors), and derived fields; latency remains a warning (`131.30s` vs `36.98s`, ~`3.55x`) and 4o still returns `429 insufficient_quota`.
- **LLM / Smart Update G4 Stage Benchmark**: added `scripts/inspect/benchmark_smart_update_g4_stages.py`, a single-fixture staged benchmark for the real Smart Update create path. It compares current Smart Update `Gemma 3` baseline with `Smart Update G4 variant 2 + lollipop-light` across source evidence, create bundle fields, raw facts, `facts_text_clean`, lollipop bucket/weight/lead/layout/writer_pack, final writer, derived `short_description`/`search_digest`, fact coverage, and per-stage timings. First evidence (`artifacts/codex/smart_update_g4_stage_benchmark_20260502T072137Z.{md,json}` on `RED-COSMOS-7902`) shows: raw facts `12 vs 12`, grounded baseline coverage `10/12` with two minor losses, lollipop-light writer_pack restored three semantic headings, final Gemma 4 writer fallback produced a non-empty structured text after `4o` returned `429 insufficient_quota`, but derived fields were empty and latency regressed hard (`36.98s` baseline vs `245.23s` candidate; biggest blockers `create_bundle_g4`, `search_digest_g4`, `short_description_g4`). Status: diagnostic only, not accepted for rollout.
- **LLM / Lollipop G4 Benchmark Diagnostics**: made `smart_update_lollipop_lab.full_cascade.run_full_cascade_variant` fail open when the final `writer.final_4o` call errors (for example OpenAI `429`). The benchmark now keeps upstream Gemma/lollipop stage payloads, writer pack, timings, and a visible `writer.final_4o.error:*` validation error instead of aborting before writing an artifact. The local `_ask_4o_json` helper now records structured OpenAI HTTP error details (`type`, `code`, message, `Retry-After`, rate-limit headers) and uses bounded jittered retry for retryable 429s, while avoiding blind retry for `insufficient_quota`. Layout cleanup now also drops invalid one-letter/generic headings before writer_pack so a model typo like `s` does not become an exact heading contract.
- **Docs / Smart Update G4 Variant 2 Benchmark Plan**: reframed the Smart Update Gemma 4 migration docs around the real production Smart Update path, not an abstract writer swap. The canonical target now states that the candidate path must remove live Gemma 3 calls, while the final public writer may be either a Gemma 4 writer lane or an explicit final 4o lane chosen by measured output quality. Documented the selected next path as variant 2 (`G4 parity Smart Update + lollipop-light description`) and pinned the risk that lollipop layout/writer_pack must not lose baseline writer strengths (epigraphs, semantic headings, lists, style, logistics separation). Added a professional stage-by-stage benchmark protocol: source evidence parity, field/merge safety, fact extraction coverage, Smart Update buckets, lollipop-light layout/writer_pack, final description, derived public fields, latency/cost, single-fixture gates before five-fixture expansion, and mandatory writer-lane reporting.
- **LLM / Lollipop Legacy Full-Source Fact Benchmark**: corrected the five static `lollipop_legacy.v14` benchmark fixtures to use full Telegram post snapshots instead of shortened source excerpts (`AUDIO-WALK`, `SACRED-LECTURE`, `WORLD-HOBBIES`, `RED-COSMOS`; `PETER-FLEET` was already corrected in the previous pass), and made Telegram public-page extraction target the exact `data-post` via `?embed=1&mode=tme` instead of the first message on `t.me/s/...`. The markdown report now renders source excerpts with a larger limit for manual audit. Gemma 4 extraction prompt was tightened for dense audio-route / lecture / object-exhibition posts, preserving participant-control details, source/methodology, craft/object attributes, and qualitative source-local facts without deterministic semantic repair. The fact-coverage reviewer timeout is now larger and has a benchmark-only 4o fallback for reviewer timeouts; generation payloads remain baseline-free. Latest full-source evidence (`artifacts/codex/lollipop_g4_benchmark_20260501T212029Z.{md,json}`): `55/56` grounded Gemma 3 baseline facts covered, `0` critical/major losses, `3/5` accepted (`PETER`, `SACRED`, `WORLD`), `2/5` partial (`AUDIO` due suspicious/fragmentary G4 facts despite 14/14 baseline coverage; `RED` due one minor craft-quality fact loss). Latency remains not accepted (`8.7x..11.4x` vs baseline), so this is a fact-layer recovery artifact, not rollout evidence.
- **LLM / Lollipop Legacy v14 Fact-Coverage Reviewer**: bumped `LEGACY_CONTRACT_VERSION` to `lollipop_legacy.v14` and added an LLM-first fact-coverage reviewer to the benchmark. After Gemma 4 extracts public/logistics facts and the writer runs, a separate Gemma 4 reviewer reads the source excerpt, the Gemma 3 baseline facts, and the Gemma 4 facts, and judges per-fact grounding, baseline coverage, useful additions, and suspicious facts; output is a structured `coverage_summary` (public/logistics/named_entity/format_topic_program/overall_verdict) plus `lost_baseline_facts[]` / `added_g4_facts[]` / `suspicious_g4_facts[]`. The reviewer is benchmark-only — baseline facts are allowed in its payload, but the extractor and writer payloads still receive zero baseline text/facts (regression-pinned by `test_lollipop_legacy_reviewer_payload_uses_baseline_facts_but_generation_does_not`). A deterministic verdict floor cross-checks the LLM judgement: a single critical loss of a grounded baseline fact, or three+ ungrounded G4 facts, force `rejected`. Markdown reports now expose every fact surface needed for manual audit: raw Gemma 3 `per_source_facts`, filtered baseline `facts_text_clean`, metadata anchors, and exact Gemma 4 public/logistics facts. The `PETER-FLEET-LECTURE-5600` fixture was corrected from a shortened manual excerpt to the full post snapshot; the earlier `T095915Z` fact-coverage artifact is therefore superseded for that fixture. Latest evidence (`artifacts/codex/lollipop_g4_benchmark_20260501T105522Z.{md,json}`): 4/5 fixtures `accepted`, `PETER-FLEET` `partial` because Gemma 4 missed the grounded baseline fact that the доклад is based on service documents and personal-origin sources. No writer prompt was tuned in this pass.
- **LLM / Lollipop Legacy v13 Simplification**: rewrote the `lollipop_legacy` lab variant from the multi-stage `v7..v12` pipeline (per-source extract + enrichment + plan + paragraph-bound writer + repair) to a tight baseline-equivalent Gemma 4 path with a 4o final-writer fallback. `smart_update_lollipop_lab/legacy_writer_family.py` now exposes only `build_extraction_system_prompt`, `extraction_response_schema`, `normalize_extraction_payload`, `merge_extraction_facts`, `build_writer_system_prompt`, `writer_response_schema`, `build_writer_payload`, `apply_writer_output`, `validate_writer_output` (read-only), and `compare_to_baseline` (read-only). All `enhancement` / `enrich_v8` / `plan_v8` / `writer_v7` / `source_writer` / `source_fact` stages and helpers (`_baseline_fact_list`, `_legacy_event_fact_floor`, `_legacy_required_fact_floor`) are removed. `scripts/inspect/benchmark_lollipop_g4.py::_run_lollipop_legacy_variant` is now: per-source Gemma 4 extraction → single Gemma 4 writer → 4o final-writer fallback when Gemma 4 writer times out / errors / returns empty. No baseline text or facts enter any generation payload (Gemma 4 or 4o), no repair pass, no source-draft fallback, no regex post-processing, and `--legacy-g4-extract` is kept only as a deprecated CLI no-op. `_ask_4o_json` now reads `FOUR_4O_TOKEN` (with `FOUR_O_TOKEN` legacy fallback) and converts Gemma uppercase JSON-Schema types to OpenAI lowercase before calling structured-outputs. Latest evidence (`artifacts/codex/lollipop_g4_benchmark_20260430T201038Z.{md,json}`): `5/5` non-empty legacy outputs, `0` critical validation errors, `0` baseline leakage; `SACRED-LECTURE`, `WORLD-HOBBIES`, `RED-COSMOS` accepted on Gemma 4 alone (speed `0.97x..2.57x`); `AUDIO-WALK` and `PETER-FLEET` succeeded on the 4o fallback after Gemma 4 writer timeouts (speed `3.10x..3.20x`, recorded as warnings, not errors). Status: **partial accepted** — non-empty stability and zero baseline leakage achieved; further work needed on 4o-fallback register and Gemma 4 writer reliability for sparse sources.
- **LLM / Baseline G4 Translation Benchmark**: added a `baseline_g4` benchmark variant for a 100% LLM-first Gemma 4 baseline translation attempt: Gemma 4 native-schema fact extraction, Gemma 4 writer, Gemma 4 reviewer, no Gemma 3 generation fallback, and no deterministic semantic text repair. The harness now renders a Baseline vs Baseline G4 comparison table with chars, quality, validation, speed ratio, and Gemma calls. Current evidence is **not accepted as complete**: `AUDIO-WALK-QUARTER-971` can pass with `errors=0` and reviewer `accepted`, but `RED-COSMOS-7902` remains a blocker due Gemma 4 writer timeout/repetition/dry-copy instability on thin object-list sources.
- **LLM / Lollipop Legacy v7 Gemma 4-only Contract**: replaced the failed `v3` source-draft/repair experiments with a stricter `lollipop_legacy.v7` path: Gemma 4 `facts_v7` extracts source-derived public/logistics facts, Gemma 4 `writer_v7` now returns paragraph records bound to `public_fact_indexes` / `logistics_fact_indexes`, and `description_md` is assembled deterministically without rewriting. Baseline text/facts, source draft, text repair, and fallback are all absent from generation. Added source-span fact checks, source-fidelity comparison against the Gemma 3 baseline, filler/adjacent-stem guards, and `length.below_public_min` for empty public copy. Regression tests cover the two-call Gemma 4-only route, source-fidelity quality delta, and validation behavior. Latest five-fixture evidence (`lollipop_g4_benchmark_20260430T153849Z`) is boundary-correct but **not accepted** as a quality replacement: `5/5` pass latency and `0` repair/fallback, but the stricter contract over-compresses source facts; `AUDIO-WALK-QUARTER-971`, `SACRED-LECTURE-ZYGMONT-3170`, and `RED-COSMOS-7902` still have validation errors, while the remaining rows are too thin for public-copy rollout.
- **LLM / Lollipop Legacy v3 Audit Fix**: corrected `lollipop_legacy` from the baseline-assisted `v2` prototype to a real Gemma 4-only lab candidate. The benchmark generation path now records `generation_uses_baseline=false`, `uses_baseline_fact_floor=false`, `includes_baseline_stage=false`, and `writer_fallback_to_baseline=false`; baseline is comparison-only and never enters source-facts, writer, repair, or fallback payloads. Added Gemma 4 `source_facts.v3`, source-draft timeout fallback, provider `500 Internal` retry handling, and regression tests that fail if baseline facts/description are sent into legacy generation. Current five-fixture evidence (`lollipop_g4_benchmark_20260430T141614Z`) is honest but **not accepted**: `3/5` improved, `2/5` regressed, and `3/5` exceed the `<=3x` latency gate, so `v3` is a corrected boundary plus failing quality baseline, not a pinned release.
- **Docs / Lollipop Legacy Version Pin**: historically pinned `lollipop_legacy.v2` in the canonical funnel doc with git tag `lollipop-legacy-v2` and acceptance artifact `lollipop_g4_benchmark_20260430T115455Z`; after the v3 audit this entry is treated as the baseline-assisted prototype record, not as the Gemma 4-only accepted contract.
- **LLM / Lollipop Legacy Prompt + Quality Tuning**: added a narrator-frame lead ban (`Погружение в ...`, `Знакомство с ...`, `Путешествие в мир ...`, `Прогулка по миру ...`, `Окунитесь в ...`, `Откройте для себя ...`, `Добро пожаловать в ...`, `Приготовьтесь ...`) plus concrete positive lead exemplars to the `lollipop_legacy.v2` writer prompt, so the editor opens with a grounded object/actor/quote/event-action instead of a stock copywriter narrator pose. The objective `compare_to_baseline` guard now treats a narrator-frame opening as a regression when the baseline did not have one, rewards narrator-frame avoidance, and emits soft warnings for `quality.lost_baseline_headings` (≥2 baseline `### ` blocks collapsed to none) and `quality.lost_baseline_epigraph` (baseline `>` opener dropped). Validator now also rejects the recurring Gemma 4 `,, ` double-comma artifact as `text.double_comma`. After the prompt + guard tightening on the same five-fixture pack, every fixture remains `quality_delta_status=improved`, narrator-frame leads disappear in `peter_fleet_lecture` and `red_cosmos`, the dropped `### ` structure is restored on `audio_walk` (1 heading) / `peter_fleet_lecture` (3) / `sacred_lecture` (2), the writer repair pass reliably fixes one observed double-comma artifact in `peter_fleet_lecture`, and all five outputs stay inside the `1.0 < speed_ratio <= 3.0` gate (max 2.84). Captured in `artifacts/codex/lollipop_g4_benchmark_20260430T115455Z.{md,json}`.
- **LLM / Lollipop Legacy Lab**: added and tightened the experimental `lollipop_legacy.v2` benchmark variant for Smart Update copy. It runs the current Gemma 3 baseline first and counts that stage in timing, but now treats non-logistics baseline facts as the mandatory public floor while keeping date/time/ticket/address facts as compact logistics context instead of forcing them into narrative prose. Gemma 4 31b runs a bounded baseline-editor pass with an objective no-worse quality gate, optional repair pass for validation/quality regressions, and baseline fallback only after repair failure or timeout. The benchmark harness supports `--variants baseline,lollipop_legacy`, ships five real-post fixtures, records the stricter `1.0 < speed_ratio <= 3.0` gate, validates style/promo/report leakage plus duplicate word/tail artifacts, and reports `quality_delta_vs_baseline`.
- **Incident / 80 Stories Source Coverage**: opened `INC-2026-05-05-80-stories-source-coverage` after `80 историй о главном` concrete event posts were weakly accumulated from `@kraftmarket39` and VK. VK crawl now preserves Russian month-name dates such as `16 мая 2026 г. в 16:00`, fails open to the normal LLM import path for event-like posts with uncertain timestamp hints, and Telegram Monitoring routes festival program-like posts through the main LLM extractor instead of immediate schedule suppression.
- **Incident / Event Source Media Aggregation**: opened `INC-2026-05-05-event-source-media-aggregation-gap` after `Куплю гараж. Калининград` showed four sources but only one Telegraph image. Event Telegraph rebuild now best-effort rehydrates missing posters from attached Telegram/VK `event_source` rows and persists unique images into `eventposter`/`event.photo_urls`.
- **Incident / 80 Stories Video Promo Gap**: opened `INC-2026-05-05-80-stories-video-promo-gap` to track the separate product gap that `80 историй о главном` had no meaningful CherryFlash/CrumpleVideo public exposure; selection-policy repair is deferred to the future `promo` feature after source backfill.
- **Incident / Smart Update Gemma 3 Fallback**: opened `INC-2026-05-05-smart-update-gemma3-fallback-hallucination` after the `80 историй` backfill exposed stale `gemma-3-27b-it` model configuration and an unsafe writer fallback attempt; source backfill was rerun in deterministic-safe mode, while model-chain/fallback repair remains a dedicated follow-up.
- **Incident / Kitoboya Garage Exhibition Date**: opened `INC-2026-05-05-kitoboya-garage-date` after production showed three active `Куплю гараж. Калининград` cards dated from 1/2 May instead of the source-confirmed 13 May opening. Telegram Monitoring and VK extraction prompts now fail closed on exhibition/fair teasers without exact day/range/end date, Smart Update skips unsupported exhibition teaser dates, `кураторские экскурсии` no longer trip the broad course-promo guard on a dated ticketed exhibition announcement, inferred long-run exhibition ranges can be corrected by a later source-grounded date, and incident closure docs now require raw-source replay through import + Smart Update for source-import quality incidents.
- **Incident / Event Quality Regression**: opened `INC-2026-05-05-event-quality-regression` after production still showed duplicate active event cards, prose/default location drift, and false free labels. Smart Update, VK intake, and Telegram import now fail closed for these classes: zero ticket price no longer implies `is_free=true`, giveaway/included-entry/positive-price evidence clears false free, library venue defaults no longer imply free without explicit free wording, rental/booking availability posts skip as non-events, unsupported Telegram locations no longer fall back to unrelated `default_location`, Kaggle preserves `default_location` as source context instead of writing it into empty venue fields, and same specific ticket/date/place or near-identical same-slot source text now merge before creating duplicates.
- **Incident / CherryFlash Disk Full**: opened `INC-2026-05-05-cherryflash-disk-full` after the 2026-05-05 CherryFlash popularity slot hit `sqlite3.OperationalError: database or disk is full` with Fly `/data` at `100%`; Guide monitoring result persistence now prunes old `/data/guide_monitoring_results/guide-excursions-*` bundles by age/count/size/free-space guard before and after each new copy so recovery artifacts cannot fill the SQLite volume again.
- **Reference / Pre-Daily Locations**: added/corrected references for the confirmed `INC-2026-05-02-pre-daily-event-quality` venues: `Винный факультет`, `РК Планета`, `Библиотека им. Лунина`, and `Замок Лабиау`; fixed the stale `винный факультет` alias and the `Библиротека` typo so future imports can ground these venues directly.
- **Incident / Pre-Daily Event Quality**: opened `INC-2026-05-02-pre-daily-event-quality` after a fresh production audit before the 2026-05-02 daily announcement found future/today duplicate cards, wrong inherited venues (`Бар Советов`, `Стендап клуб Локация`, `Понарт`), and placeholder/prose venue fields. Production rows were repaired with row-level backups, affected Telegraph event pages were rebuilt without rerunning daily, and Telegram/VK extraction prompts now require event-local venue grounding, reject literal field-name placeholders, and preserve canonical ticket/program titles over in-character promo copy.
- **Incident / Future Event Quality Audit**: opened and mitigated `INC-2026-05-01-future-event-quality-audit` after a fresh production snapshot confirmed future active events with prose/schedule fragments in `location_name` and duplicate active cards for Bar Bastion, Locostandup, tango, and film-schedule repost cases. Production data was repaired, affected event Telegraph pages were rebuilt, Telegram candidate import now drops prose-like `location_address` fragments while recovering known venues, the reference layer gained the confirmed future-event venues, month/weekend permanent-exhibition sections now stay compact and bounded while the month splitter handles oversized exhibition tails instead of hitting Telegraph `CONTENT_TOO_BIG`, and month nav-refresh fallback rebuilds no longer recursively trigger another full nav refresh.
- **Incident / Daily Location Drift**: opened `INC-2026-05-01-daily-location-drift` after the 2026-05-01 daily announcement exposed prose/schedule fragments in `location_name`, unsupported extracted times creating doubtful duplicates, a stale VK City Jazz default, and missing venue references. Telegram Monitoring now adds an LLM-first Gemma venue-review pass for suspicious venue fields, Telegram candidate import marks unsupported extracted times as weak `time_is_default` anchors, known VK source defaults are repaired away from `Калининград Сити Джаз Клуб`, and the reference layer gained `Сырный магазин Камамбер`, `Бар ЛЕС`, and the Gusev railway station aliases.
- **Incident / Telegram Monitoring Event Quality Regressions**: opened `INC-2026-04-30-tg-monitoring-event-quality-regressions` after the 2026-04-30 Telegram batch produced work-hours event cards, duplicate public cards, and paid/unknown-price events marked as free. The LLM-first policy now explicitly covers semantic fields such as `is_free`, `ticket_status`, work-hours/non-event classification, venue/title meaning, and duplicate/match decisions; Telegram Monitoring Gemma prompts/schema now state that missing price is unknown rather than free, ticket links/status/sale wording require explicit free-entry evidence before `is_free=true`, and schedule rescue must return `[]` for institution work-hours notices. A follow-up production audit of all active future free-labeled events cleared `is_free` on 69 additional rows without explicit free-attendance evidence, leaving only explicit-free or explicit-free-with-registration rows marked free.
- **Incident / Telegram Monitoring Work Schedule False Skips**: opened `INC-2026-04-30-tg-monitoring-work-schedule-false-skips` after `@kenigatom/496` and `@kraftmarket39/199` were extracted by Telegram Monitoring but skipped as `skipped_non_event:work_schedule`; the Gemma extract prompt now explicitly keeps lectures/shows/festival slots at museum/library venues importable, while Smart Update limits its deterministic guard to explicit work-hours/closure wording and no longer infers schedule notices from venue words plus dates.
- **Reference / Location Audit**: added checked venue references and aliases for `Бар Ельцин`, `Частная школа Дирижабль`, `Стендап клуб Локация`, `Бар Чилинтано`, `ДК Машиностроитель`, `Виштынецкий эколого-исторический музей`, and Зеленоградский ГЦКИ after the 2026-04-28 fresh-event location audit found missing addresses and prose-fragment venue drift in Telegram imports.
- **Guide Excursions / Source Catalog**: added `@murnikovaT` to the canonical guide monitoring source seed as a personal Kaliningrad guide channel.
- **Incident / VK Smart Update False Skips**: opened `INC-2026-04-28-vk-smart-update-false-skips` after two normal VK events were rejected as `skipped_festival_post` and `skipped_non_event:online_event`; Smart Update no longer treats `онлайн-регистрация` as online-only, festival routing rescues a single concrete event inside a cycle/program from `festival_post` even when the post has bullet-listed materials/conditions, and the event-parse prompt now distinguishes whole-festival posts from `event_with_festival` single events.
- **Incident / CherryFlash Missing Rehydrated Posters**: opened `INC-2026-04-27-cherryflash-missing-photo-urls` after the scheduled CherryFlash loop repeatedly failed before Kaggle because selected events had in-memory rehydrated posters but empty persisted `photo_urls`; selection now writes rehydrated Telegram/VK poster URLs back to the event row before session/render payload creation.
- **CherryFlash / Rehydrated Poster SQLite Lock Guard**: rehydrated poster persistence now retries transient SQLite lock failures and, if the repair still cannot be made durable, skips only that candidate instead of crashing the entire popularity selection or selecting an event that will reload without photos.
- **CherryFlash / Business Story Secret Cohesion**: CherryFlash now co-locates encrypted story secrets with the per-run `cherryflash-session-*` dataset and marks configured Telegram Business targets as blocking/required, so a missing Business connection secret fails preflight before render instead of silently producing an incomplete personal-account fanout.
- **CherryFlash / Stale Kaggle Bundle Guard**: CherryFlash kernel deploys now prune older `cherryflash-session-*` dataset sources, require Kaggle metadata to confirm the fresh session dataset before persisting handoff, and log story runtime config/secrets matching inside Kaggle so stale mounted bundles cannot masquerade as a successful daily run.
- **CherryFlash / Scheduled Runner Visibility**: scheduled `popular_review` now fails `ops_run(kind='video_popular_review')` if the pipeline returns without creating a session, instead of recording a misleading success for a no-op catch-up.
- **Telegram Business Stories / Profile Page Visibility**: Business story posts from CherryFlash and `/check_business` now send Bot API `post_to_chat_page=true`, so accepted stories are intended for the account page/profile story list, not only the active story ring.
- **Incident / Prod Unresponsive During CherryFlash Recovery**: opened `INC-2026-04-27-prod-unresponsive-during-cherryflash-recovery` and made production health/webhook readiness a required closure check for long CherryFlash catch-up validation.
- **Incident / Telegram Monitoring Sticky Skipped Post**: opened `INC-2026-04-27-tg-monitoring-sticky-skipped-post` after `@kraftmarket39/193` was extracted by Kaggle but stored as `telegram_scanned_message.status=skipped` with `events_imported=0`, keeping a valid May 15 lecture out of `/daily` and video-announcement inventory. Telegram Monitoring now reprocesses legacy incomplete skipped/partial/error scan rows when they have no diagnostic reason and no attached `event_source`, while new incomplete skips persist a compact `skip_breakdown` so intentional skips remain terminal. Telegram import also treats a zero extracted ticket price as free even when the LLM returned `is_free=false`, covering `80 историй о главном` posts where Telegram custom emoji `🆓` can be stripped before extraction.
- **Telegram Monitoring / Remote Session Guard**: Kaggle `CANCEL_ACKNOWLEDGED` and `CANCELED` states are now treated as terminal by the shared remote Telegram session guard, so a manually cancelled monitoring kernel no longer blocks the next catch-up as `remote_telegram_session_busy`.
- **Telegram Sources / `@ecoklgd`**: added `@ecoklgd` to the canonical Telegram Monitoring source catalog and fixed `normalize_tg_username()` so public `t.me/...` and `tg://resolve?domain=...` links normalize to usernames instead of being rejected by double-escaped URL patterns.
- **Telegram Business Stories / `/check_business` Tester**: added admin-only `/check_business` that lists every cached Business connection with `is_enabled=True` and `can_manage_stories=True`, labels rows with `@username` (fallback to `hash:<8>` when username is missing), and on click puts the operator into a 10-minute photo session — the next image (photo or `image/*` document, `/cancel` aborts) is uploaded via Bot API `postStory` (`active_period=21600`) on behalf of the chosen partner so the operator can verify a connection end-to-end without waiting for a CherryFlash slot.
- **Telegram Business Stories / Cache Write Hotfix**: `cache_business_connection` no longer crashes on the live aiogram update because `BusinessConnection.date` is now coerced from `datetime` to a unix timestamp before JSON-encoding, so the Fernet-encrypted store is actually written on every webhook event. `load_cached_business_connections` skips entries that fail to decrypt with a warning instead of taking the whole story-secrets build down. The superadmin now receives a DM from the production bot whenever a Business connection is newly cached or its `is_enabled`/`can_manage_stories` state changes (both via the dedicated `business_connection` event and via the `business_message` recovery path), so connection health is visible without inspecting `/data`.
- **Incident / CrumpleVideo Required Story Fanout**: opened `INC-2026-04-26-crumple-story-required-channel-fanout` after the scheduled CrumpleVideo story published only to `@lovekenig` while `@kenigevents` returned `BOOSTS_REQUIRED`; story targets now distinguish the self-account render gate from required channel fanout, so `me` can keep the render alive while missing required channels make final story publish fail instead of ending green.
- **Telegram Business Stories / Existing Connection Recovery**: webhook updates now include `business_message` / `edited_business_message`, and those updates recover and encrypted-cache `business_connection_id` through `getBusinessConnection`, so already-connected Business accounts can be captured without reconnecting the chatbot.
- **CherryFlash / Business Story Target Storage**: personal Telegram Business story targets now come from runtime DB setting `video_announce_story_business_targets` instead of repo env/code, while `story_publish.json` still carries only hash labels and encrypted story secrets carry the Bot API connection data.
- **CherryFlash / Telegram Business Stories Fanout**: scheduled CherryFlash story publish now appends encrypted Telegram Business targets after the existing Telethon channel-story chain, keeps `600s` spacing between all targets, posts Business stories through Bot API `postStory`, and stores only hash labels in config/docs while `business_connection_id` stays inside encrypted story secrets.
- **Codex / Telegram Business Stories Skill**: added the project-local `.codex/skills/telegram-business-stories` playbook for safe Business Bot API story publishing, webhook/capture recovery, encrypted connection-cache handling, and prod verification.
- **Telegram Business Stories / Webhook Contract**: production webhook now includes `business_connection`, startup uses a canonical allowed-updates list, and incoming Business connections are cached only as Fernet-encrypted payloads with hash-only logs so story publishing no longer requires a manual capture window after connection changes.
- **Incident / Prod Slow During VK Daily Catch-Up**: opened `INC-2026-04-26-prod-slow-during-vk-daily-catchup` after a manual full-bot VK daily catch-up attempt on the serving Fly machine slowed `/healthz` and produced `/webhook` proxy errors; recovery now records the safer compensation path and adds a follow-up for a lightweight VK daily catch-up tool/runbook.
